Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献有两点：一是提出一种将未预期故障恢复从"检测"扩展到"缓解"的架构，即确定性控制与可按需调用的 LLM 诊断恢复规划相结合；二是提出用于评估低功耗 AUV 上 LLM 辅助任务管理的集成评估方法论。其意义在于为资源受限平台上的 LLM 辅助自主性提供了可复现、可量化的评测范式，并揭示了前沿模型与本地模型在诊断可靠性上的显著差距以及"诊断—决策解耦"现象。潜在局限在于：评估局限于单一 mass-shift 故障类型与仿真环境，未涉及真实水下部署；未报告具体模型名称、推理延迟/算力开销等低功耗部署关键指标，且 LLM-as-judge 打分的可靠性本身也依赖评判模型。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating beyond reliable communications must recover from failures without human intervention. We investigate an architecture in which conventional deterministic layered control autonomy manages normal operations, while an invokable large language model (LLM) serves as a diagnostic and recovery planner when onboard anomaly detection identifies performance outside expected limits. Because language models are stochastic, rigorous evaluation requires ensemble testing rather than individual demonstrations. We present a closed-loop simulation architecture that couples real-time C vehicle software with a higher-level orchestration layer for physics-based fault injection, structured prompting, language-model interaction, mission file generation, validation, execution, and LLM-judge scoring. The framework, which we call SPAR (Simulation Platform for AUV Recovery), supports evaluation across fault realizations, prompt structures, reasoning models, and mission conditions. We vary these for a mass-shift fault over 480 SPAR trials, evaluating a frontier model and three off-the-shelf locally deployable LLMs. Model choice dominates diagnosis: the frontier model places the CG-shift mechanism in its top three hypotheses in 85-90% of trials, versus 60-78% for the best local model. Reasoning analysis indicates that local-model success is associated with following the complete diagnostic procedure, whereas weaker models often commit prematurely to elevator failure even though the actuator tracks its command. Diagnosis and operational decision performance do not appear to be coupled in this dataset. The contributions are an architecture extending unanticipated-fault recovery from detection to mitigation and an ensemble methodology for evaluating LLM-assisted mission management on low-power AUVs.

</details>


### [199] [SmellDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Quadruped Navigation with Olfactory Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20624)
*Faith Ogunwoye,Iana Zhura,Hajira Amjad,Timofei Kozlov,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Fedor Fedorov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmellDiffusion 是一个面向"命名气体泄漏搜索"任务的仿真流水线，用开放词汇嗅觉场景图表示特定气体的分布区域，并让经典规划器与扩散规划器共享同一目标。其核心是峰值局部几何门控（用于选择性源校正）和气体引导的扩散轨迹生成，在位移峰值场景下显著降低源估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人被派往搜寻"指定名称"的气体泄漏时，需要同时保持气体身份识别、估计源位置并导航到目标。然而在真实流场中，浓度峰值常因风向/流场效应偏离真实源位置（论文中424个源-风配置中有28个峰值与源距离超过0.5m），若机器人直接以浓度峰为目标，会导致源定位误差大。因此需要一种能识别"峰值-源位移"并进行选择性校正的机制，同时兼顾实时导航与气体暴露最小化。

Method: 1) 表示层：在开放词汇嗅觉场景图中编码物种特异的气体区域，使经典规划器与扩散规划器共享同一场景图源估计作为导航目标。2) 峰值局部几何门控：一个与具体气体源无关（source-independent）的几何门控模块，仅在训练集上校准，在观测峰值处判断是否存在位移，若触发则启用预计算的前向匹配（forward-matching）校正，避免对所有样本一律校正。3) 气体引导的扩散规划：以扩散模型生成气体引导的轨迹，与气体引导A*形成对照。

Result: 在424个已求解流场的源-风配置中，28个存在峰值位移>0.5m。几何门控在10个留出位移案例中检出9个，精确率0.64；门控触发时用前向匹配校正，仅对14/204个案例使用匹配，位移案例平均误差从1.468m降至0.592m（降60%），全案例平均误差从0.205m降至0.180m。受控对比中，best-of-ten扩散的平均气体暴露（0.0476）与气体引导A*（0.0455）相当；单次扩散提议耗时41.7ms，气体引导A*为72.3ms，但best-of-ten顺序采样会增加总运行时间。普通A*同样能到达目标，且是最快、路径最短的方法。6次匹配的Gazebo实验中，机器人到源的平均误差为A* 0.39m、扩散0.31m。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一个结合开放词汇嗅觉场景图、峰值几何门控校正与扩散规划的气体源搜索仿真框架，其贡献在于以低成本选择性校正在流场中常见的峰值-源位移，并在源误差、气体暴露与运行时间之间取得平衡。局限方面：扩散的best-of-ten顺序采样带来额外总运行时间；几何门控精确率0.64，仍存在误报；实验主要在仿真与有限（6次）Gazebo验证中进行，真实开放环境泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: A robot sent to a named gas leak must preserve gas identity, estimate the source, and navigate to the resulting goal. We present SmellDiffusion, a simulation pipeline that represents species-specific gas zones in an open-vocabulary olfactory scene graph and shares the selected goal between classical and diffusion planners. Its key components are a peak-local geometric gate for selective source correction and diffusion-based, gas-guided trajectory generation. Among 424 unique source-wind configurations in solved flow, 28 have a concentration peak displaced more than 0.5m from the source. A source-independent geometric gate, calibrated only on the training split and evaluated at the observed peak, detects 9 of 10 held-out displacements at 0.64 precision. Gating a precomputed forward-matching correction reduces mean error on the displaced cases from 1.468m to 0.592m (60%), using matching for only 14/204 cases. All-case mean error falls from 0.205m to 0.180m. All planners receive the same scene-graph source estimate as their goal. In a controlled comparison, best-of-ten diffusion achieves mean gas exposure comparable to gas-guided A* (0.0476 versus 0.0455). A single diffusion proposal takes 41.7ms, compared with 72.3ms for gas-guided A*, although best-of-ten sequential sampling increases total runtime. Plain A* also reaches the same goal and remains the fastest and shortest-path method. Six matched Gazebo runs give mean robot-to-source errors of 0.39m for A* and 0.31m for diffusion.

</details>


### [200] [RTK-Vision PPO for Autonomous Micro UAV Recovery on an Airborne Carrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20629)
*Aashish Sahu,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种RTK-视觉引导的强化学习框架，使微型子无人机能够从飞行中的大型母机（空中载体）起飞、执行独立任务、返回母机当前位置并重新对接锁定。终端回收阶段由PPO策略控制，结合RTK-GNSS与下视相机标记检测，在MuJoCo中训练后迁移到真实硬件，实现了完整的"空中释放-任务-空中回收"自主循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用无人机的"释放-任务-回收"循环依赖自主空中回收能力，但该问题耦合了长距离会合、近距离感知、载体运动、气动干扰以及不连续的接触事件，难度极高。

Method: 采用RTK-视觉引导的强化学习框架：子机与母机均搭载RTK-GNSS，母机持续共享导航状态；接近回收平台时，RTK保持工作，同时下视相机的基准标记（fiducial marker）检测器提供标记相对对齐线索。终端回收阶段的PPO策略在基于物理的MuJoCo仿真中训练，显式建模传感器噪声、气动扰动代理模型以及标记延迟随机化，随后迁移到真机。PX4负责底层稳定，另设确定性安全门独立于学习策略授权下降。

Result: PPO检查点在2000个保留的随机化终端回合中成功率达99.55%，相同条件下调优PD基线为78.4%，平面终端误差中位数6.62 cm。14次户外试验中完整任务成功13次（92.9%），涵盖近区回收以及母机远离释放点后的回收场景。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是展示了完整的自主空中部署与回收循环，而非孤立的降落机动，为巡检、监视和移动物流中的可复用母机-子机协同作业奠定实用基础。局限在于：终端回收依赖基准标记与RTK，安全门限制了策略权限，且户外验证规模较小（14次试验）。

Abstract: Autonomous recovery of a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) onto a moving airborne carrier enables reusable deploy-mission-recover operation, but couples long-range rendezvous, close-range perception, carrier motion, aerodynamic interaction, and a discontinuous contact event. This paper presents an RTK-vision-guided reinforcement-learning framework in which a child UAV is physically transported by a larger carrier, takes off from the carrier while airborne, executes an independent sortie, returns to the carrier's current position, redocks, and subsequently descends with the carrier. Both vehicles carry RTK-GNSS, and the carrier continuously shares its navigation state with the child. Near the recovery deck, RTK remains active while a downward-facing camera with a fiducial marker detector provides marker-relative alignment cues. A proximal policy optimization (PPO) policy governing the terminal recovery phase is trained in a physics-based MuJoCo simulation environment with explicit sensor noise models, an aerodynamic disturbance surrogate, and marker-latency randomization, then transferred to hardware. PX4 retains low-level stabilization, and a deterministic safety gate authorizes descent independently of the learned policy. The PPO checkpoint achieves 99.55% success over 2,000 held-out randomized terminal episodes, compared with 78.4% for a tuned PD baseline under identical conditions, with a median planar terminal error of 6.62 cm. Across 14 outdoor trials, the full mission succeeds in 13 trials (92.9%), spanning both near-region recovery and recovery after the carrier translates away from the release point. The results demonstrate a complete autonomous aerial deployment-and-recovery cycle rather than an isolated landing maneuver, establishing a practical basis for reusable carrier-child operation in inspection, surveillance, and mobile-logistics applications.

</details>


### [201] [TraceFlow: Guiding Frozen Flow-Matching Robot Policies with Success and Failure Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20646)
*Jiaxuan Zhang,Ruizhe Liu,Yu Zhang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TraceFlow 提出一种"进度对齐引导场"（progress-aligned guidance field），将检索到的成功/失败 rollout 的动作密度转化为对冻结流匹配（flow-matching）VLA 策略的有界修正；仅用每条 rollout 的一个终态结果比特（成功/失败）作为监督信号。配套的 TraceBank 存储按时间排序的状态-动作轨迹及其终态标签，从目标任务的训练轨迹起步，并逐步纳入部署机器人自身的 rollout。在无权重更新的前提下，真实机器人顺序装箱任务成功率从 21/50 提升到 39/50，第 1 轮堆叠后达到 47/50，错序 episodes 由 20 降至 0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略（含 flow-matching action expert）通过积分学习到的速度场生成动作块，权重固定后，先前 rollout 的成败无法改变当前生成的 chunk，即策略缺乏在线自适应能力。现有测试时方法通过检索成功样本、学习 critic、verifier 或动力学模型来为冻结策略提供额外输入，但均未将机器人自身的失败 rollout 作为负向证据利用，且这些方法通常依赖结果比特之外的标注（如奖励、价值标签、失败类型）。本文关注的核心问题是：能否仅凭"每段 rollout 一个终态结果比特"这一极弱监督，同时利用成功与失败轨迹来校正冻结策略，尤其针对长时程、有顺序约束的任务（如顺序装箱/堆叠），因为此类任务的失败往往源于时序错乱而非单步动作错误。

Method: 方法核心是把检索到的成功与失败 rollout 的动作分布构造为对冻结 flow-matching action expert 的引导场，且该修正有界（bounded correction），避免偏离基策略过快。引导为"进度对齐"（progress-aligned）：按轨迹内的相对时间/进度阶段对齐不同 rollout 的动作密度，使不同长度、不同时间尺度上的轨迹可比较与聚合。监督信号仅为一个终态结果比特（无中间奖励、无失败类型标签）。TraceBank 作为记忆库存储 trace（按时间排序的 state-action 记录 + 终态标签），初始化自目标任务的训练 traces，并在部署过程中持续纳入机器人自身 rollout，从而实现"无权重更新"的在线适应。检索与引导密度之比不做显式分配控制，文中指出 bank 的成功/失败比并不能直接预测检索分配。

Result: 真实机器人有序装箱任务：基策略按序完成 21/50 试次，TraceFlow 为 39/50，再经一轮堆叠（无权重更新）达 47/50，错序 episodes 由 20 降至 0。仿真中增益具有选择性：在使用 per-suite 选定配置时，RoboMemArena 的 Sequence 任务成功率由 78.92% 提升至 91.50%，Transferring 在堆叠第 2 轮由 54.41% 提升至 62.00%；26 任务总体指标基本不变，Counting 与 Occlusion 分别下降 1.12 和 1.42 个百分点，LIBERO-Plus (Long) 变化 +1.27 个百分点（p = 0.0733，未达显著）。堆叠增益有限且每个分支在第 10 轮之前即达到峰值，说明性能提升存在饱和；同时 bank 的成功/失败比例无法预测检索分配策略。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：提出以极弱监督（单比特终态结果）同时利用成功与失败 rollout 的测试时引导机制，在冻结 flow-matching VLA 策略上实现有界、进度对齐的动作修正，并通过 TraceBank 支持机器人在无权重更新下持续积累自身经验。在有顺序约束的真实机器人任务上取得显著提升且消除错序，验证了失败 rollout 作为负向证据的价值。局限包括：增益对任务具有选择性，聚合基准上无明显提升甚至个别任务下降；堆叠增益在约第 10 轮前饱和；LIBERO-Plus (Long) 提升不显著（p=0.0733）；依赖 per-suite 的超参选择，且成功/失败比无法指导检索分配，说明引导场的适用边界与自动化配置仍是开放问题。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a flow-matching action expert generates each action chunk (a short command sequence) by integrating a learned velocity field; once its weights are fixed, the success or failure of an earlier rollout cannot change the chunk generated now. Concurrent test-time methods give a frozen policy such an input from retrieved successes, a learned critic, a verifier, or a dynamics model, but none uses the robot's own failed rollouts as negative evidence with nothing but a terminal outcome bit. We introduce TraceFlow, a progress-aligned guidance field that turns the action densities of retrieved successful and failed rollouts into a bounded correction to a frozen flow-matching action expert, using one terminal outcome bit per rollout and no other label. Its TraceBank stores traces, time-ordered state-action records with a terminal label, starts from the target-task training traces, and later admits the deployed robot's own rollouts. On an ordered real-robot packing task the base completes 21 of 50 trials in order, TraceFlow 39, and one stacking round without any weight update 47, with wrong-sequence episodes falling from 20 to 0. In simulation the gain is selective: with per-suite selected settings, TraceFlow raises RoboMemArena Sequence from 78.92\% to 91.50\% task success and Transferring from 54.41\% to 62.00\% at stacking round 2, leaves the 26-task aggregate unchanged, lowers Counting and Occlusion by 1.12 and 1.42 points, and changes LIBERO-Plus (Long) by +1.27 points (p = 0.0733). Stacking gains are finite, every branch peaking before round ten, and the bank's success-to-failure ratio predicts no retrieval allocation.

</details>


### [202] [SkipVLA: Skipping VLA Steps with Classical Planning for Fast Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20648)
*Kaivalya Agrawal,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Raymond A. Yeh,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkipVLA 提出一种混合机器人策略：将预训练 VLA 模型与经典运动规划器结合，仅在抓取、放置等接触丰富技能上查询 VLA，而自由空间移动交给运动规划器，从而在保持任务成功率的同时将任务完成时间最高缩短 2.5 倍并显著降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型能将相机图像和语言指令直接映射为机器人动作，具备通用语义理解能力，但测试时推理缓慢，尤其在长时程任务中需要频繁查询策略，导致延迟高、能耗大。现有加速方案（蒸馏小模型、异步动作块重叠、搭配快速低层策略）仍需在整段任务中运行学习型策略。另一方面，经典运动规划器能快速生成无碰撞轨迹，却需要显式目标且缺乏任务语义理解。因此作者希望结合二者优势：用规划器处理自由空间运动，用 VLA 处理需要语义与接触的技能。

Method: SkipVLA 是一种混合策略架构。它复用 VLA 中冻结的视觉-语言骨干网络，为每次规划运动预测一个目标位姿（target pose），并将该预测器与经典运动规划器配合使用：自由空间运动由规划器完成，仅在抓取、放置等接触丰富技能时查询 VLA。关键创新在于目标位姿预测器无需引入额外演示数据，而是直接利用大型 VLA 已学到的表征能力进行训练，从而避免了额外的数据采集成本。整体上，VLA 只在少量接触关键步骤被调用，大幅减少推理次数。

Result: 作者在仿真环境的 13 个 LIBERO 任务上使用三种不同的 VLA 模型进行评测，并在真实 6-DoF YAM 机械臂上测试了三个拾取-放置任务。结果显示，SkipVLA 在达到与原始 VLA 相同任务成功率的前提下，任务完成速度最高提升 2.5 倍，同时能耗显著降低。实验覆盖仿真与真实机器人、多种 VLA 骨干，验证了方法的通用性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: SkipVLA 的主要贡献在于提出一种将通用 VLA 与经典运动规划器混合的策略框架，通过冻结 VLA 骨干预测目标位姿且不引入额外演示数据，显著降低长时程任务的延迟与能耗，同时保持成功率。其意义在于为 VLA 的实时部署提供了一条实用路径。潜在局限包括：方法依赖任务可被分解为自由空间运动与接触技能，对接触密集或难以显式定义规划目标的任务可能适用性有限；目标位姿预测器的精度对规划成功有较大影响。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a class of generalist robot policies that map camera images and language instructions directly to robot actions. While promising, these models remain slow at test time, particularly for long-horizon tasks that require many queries to the policy. Recent efforts reduce VLA latency by distilling smaller models, overlapping asynchronous action chunks, or pairing the VLA with a fast low-level policy, but still run a learned policy for the entire task. In contrast to VLA, classical motion planners quickly find collision-free motions, but require an explicit goal and have no semantic understanding of the task. In this work, we present SkipVLA, a hybrid policy that combines a pretrained VLA with a classical motion planner, using the planner for free-space motion and querying the VLA only for contact-rich skills such as grasping and placing. SkipVLA reuses the frozen vision-language backbone of the VLA to predict a target pose for each planned motion, and learns this predictor without additional demonstrations introduced into the system by using what was already learnt by the large VLA. We evaluate SkipVLA with three VLAs on 13 LIBERO tasks in simulation and three pick-and-place tasks on a physical 6-DoF YAM arm, demonstrating up to 2.5x faster task completion and significantly lower energy consumption while achieving the same task success rate.

</details>


### [203] [DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20649)
*Yan Qin,Yue Chen,Wenwei Lin,Shujia Liu,Chuqiao Lyu,Kailun Su,Chenze Yu,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexTouch-WM 是一种动作条件化的世界模型，利用可扩展的人类触觉数据来联合预测未来的 RGB 观测与双手（bilateral）触觉动态，从而在保持真实机器人监督数据量固定的前提下，借助人类交互数据提升灵巧操作世界模型的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习接触密集型的灵巧操作预测模型需要密集的触觉交互数据，但这类数据在真实机器人上采集成本高昂且难以规模化，同时现有触觉数据往往与特定机器人的传感器和本体（embodiment）强绑定，导致难以跨平台迁移。作者的核心动机是：如果能把人类与机器人的触觉观测和动作空间"对齐/兼容"，那么人类与机器人的操作过程可能共享可迁移的接触动力学，从而将人类触觉数据作为一种可扩展的监督来源。

Method: 1) 传感器层面：在人类手和灵巧机器人手上部署共享感知布局（shared sensing layout）的柔性压阻（piezoresistive）传感阵列，使两端的触觉观测在形式上兼容。2) 动作层面：通过将人类运动重定向（retarget）到机器人动作空间，使人类交互能够监督同一套用于真实机器人预测的动力学模型。3) 模型架构：DexTouch-WM 由一个预训练视频专家（video expert）和一个轻量级触觉专家（tactile expert）耦合而成，通过解剖结构感知的触觉 token（anatomy-aware tactile tokens）与对齐后的动作条件（aligned action conditioning）进行融合。4) 该模型是动作条件化的世界模型，联合预测未来的 RGB 观测与双侧触觉动态。5) 整体框架实现"人类到机器人"的规模化（human-to-robot scaling），即用人类交互数据补充机器人数据来训练同一动力学模型。

Result: 实验设置：在人到机器人规模化实验中，固定使用 5 小时真实机器人监督数据，将人类交互数据从 0 小时逐步增加到 100 小时。尽管人类与机器人任务集合并不重叠（disjoint），在留出的机器人域（held-out robot-domain）上，视觉、几何与接触预测均获得显著提升。除预测任务外，作者还将该世界模型作为策略评估的代理环境（surrogate environment），并用于生成合成轨迹以支持真实机器人策略学习。结果表明，可扩展的人类交互为学习灵巧机器人世界模型提供了一条互补的数据维度（data axis）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出 DexTouch-WM，一种动作条件化的多模态（RGB + 触觉）世界模型，通过共享触觉传感布局与动作重定向，将人类触觉交互作为可规模化的监督信号迁移到机器人域；验证了人类数据对机器人域预测、策略评估和策略学习的增益。意义：为灵巧操作与接触密集型任务的世界模型学习提供了一条不依赖大规模真机数据的新路径，兼具跨本体触觉迁移与数据可扩展性。潜在局限：论文摘要未明确说明模型在不同本体/不同传感器泛化时的具体失效边界、人类与机器人触觉信号之间的物理差异（如力学量纲、刚度）带来的对齐误差，以及作为代理环境和合成轨迹生成器时的策略性能上界；此外感知布局的一致性假设在更广泛机器人与传感器平台上的可扩展性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires dense tactile interaction, but such data are costly to scale on real robots and remain tied to embodiment-specific sensors. We introduce DexTouch-WM, an action-conditioned world model that learns from scalable human touch to jointly predict future RGB observations and bilateral tactile dynamics. Our insight is that human and robot manipulation share transferable contact dynamics when their tactile observations and action spaces are made compatible. We deploy flexible piezoresistive arrays with a shared sensing layout on both human and dexterous robot hands, and retarget human motion into the robot action space so that human interaction can supervise the same dynamics model used for real-robot prediction. DexTouch-WM couples a pretrained video expert with a lightweight tactile expert using anatomy-aware tactile tokens and aligned action conditioning. In human-to-robot scaling experiments, we keep five hours of real-robot supervision fixed while increasing human interaction from 0 to 100 hours, and observe substantial improvements in held-out robot-domain visual, geometric, and contact prediction despite disjoint human and robot task sets. Beyond prediction, we evaluate the world models as surrogate environments for policy evaluation and as generators of synthetic trajectories for real-robot policy learning, showing that scalable human interaction provides a complementary data axis for learning dexterous robot world models.

</details>


### [204] [HIL-UMI: Bringing Human-in-the-Loop Post-Training of Vision-Language-Action Models to Universal Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20659)
*Zimu Han,Yiming Zeng,Jiyao Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yuanfei Wang,Yixiang Jin,Shiqi Li,Shuangben Chen,Wei Huang,Ruodai Li,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIL-UMI 提出一种"免机器人"的人在回路（human-in-the-loop）VLA 后训练框架：在手持 UMI 演示过程中同步查询当前策略，用 Energy Score 检测分布外状态并触发针对性数据采集，同时用在线低优势预测精炼基于进度的优势估计器，最终以优势条件行为克隆完成策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型虽提供强先验，但适配具体部署仍困难。监督微调（SFT）依赖静态示范数据，存在两个瓶颈：(1) 静态数据对分布外（OOD）状态覆盖不足；(2) 标准模仿学习目标无法区分"有进展"的行为与无用数据。交互式后训练可缓解上述问题，但通常需要在物理机器人上反复执行策略并依赖人工干预，成本高、难以跨操作者与跨地点扩展。因此论文动机是保留人在回路学习的迭代性与策略感知特性，同时将数据采集与机器人部署解耦。

Method: HIL-UMI 基于 Universal Manipulation Interface（UMI）手持采集装置构建，核心包含两个反馈回路：
1) 策略引导采集：在人类手持 UMI 演示的同一观测流上查询当前策略，但不执行其预测动作；用 Energy Score 比较人类动作轨迹与策略推理结果，当二者差异指示处于 OOD 区域时触发数据采集。
2) 优势估计精炼：独立反馈回路中，利用在线低优势预测识别关键片段，用于精炼一个基于进度（progress-based）的优势估计器；更新后的估计器再以优势条件行为克隆（advantage-conditioned behavioral cloning）指导训练，训练数据由基础示范与新策略数据按平衡混合构成。
该方法保留了 HIL 的迭代、策略感知特性，同时实现数据采集与机器人执行的解耦。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务（覆盖长时序与精细操作）上进行实验：HIL-UMI 相比 SFT 取得一致提升，并同时受益于针对性采集（targeted collection）与优势精炼（advantage refinement）两个组件。在 Clean Up Table 任务上，HIL-UMI 优于 HG-DAgger，且单帧采集时间更低，表明该方案在跨操作者与跨地点的 VLA 后训练上具备可扩展性。（摘要未给出具体数值指标。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出免机器人的手持 UMI 人在回路 VLA 后训练框架；用 Energy Score 实现策略引导的 OOD 触发式数据采集；用优势估计器精炼与优势条件行为克隆提升模仿学习效率；在真实操作任务中优于 SFT 与 HG-DAgger 并降低采集时间。意义在于提供了可扩展、低部署成本的 VLA 适配路径。局限（据摘要推断）：仅在四个真实任务上验证，缺乏大规模多任务/多场景评估；依赖 UMI 手持装置与人工操作一致性；Energy Score 阈值与优势估计器的敏感性未在摘要中讨论。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models provide powerful priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them to a specific deployment remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on task-specific demonstrations provides a step toward deployment, but faces two persistent limitations: static data provide limited coverage of out-of-distribution states, and standard imitation objectives do not distinguish progressing behavior from less useful data. Interactive post-training can address these limitations, but typically requires repeated policy execution and human intervention on a physical robot. We introduce HIL-UMI, a policy-guided Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) framework for robot-free human-in-the-loop VLA post-training. During handheld UMI demonstrations, HIL-UMI queries the current policy on the same observation stream without executing its predictions. The Energy Score compares the human action trajectory with policy inference and triggers collection when their discrepancy indicates an out-of-distribution region. In a separate feedback loop, low online advantage predictions identify essential segments for refining a progress-based advantage estimator. The updated estimator then guides advantage-conditioned behavioral cloning using a balanced mixture of base demonstrations and new policy data. This design preserves the iterative and policy-aware nature of human-in-the-loop learning while decoupling data collection from robot deployment. Experiments on four real-world tasks spanning long-horizon and precise manipulation show that HIL-UMI achieves consistent improvement over SFT and benefits from both targeted collection and advantage refinement. Moreover, HIL-UMI outperforms HG-DAgger on Clean Up Table with lower per-frame collection time, suggesting a scalable path for VLA post-training across operators and locations.

</details>


### [205] [Learning Foresight without Explicit Trajectories for 3D Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20669)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Changbo Yan,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Movement Trend Guidance（运动趋势引导），一种为 3D 扩散策略（如 DP3）注入"交互未来走向"先验的轻量方法。通过从短观测历史中学习一个紧凑的交互演化潜变量，在训练时用稀疏的未来夹爪状态进行监督，推理时该潜变量作为面向未来的条件与当前观测一起指导动作生成。无需显式规划，仅增加 3.52% 参数即在 RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-40、DexArt 及真实机器人任务上稳定超越 DP3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 扩散策略（diffusion policy）擅长从当前观测生成几何上可行的动作，但成功的操作不仅需要"当下什么运动可行"，还需要"交互将往何处发展"的前瞻性。现有策略大多将这种前瞻能力交由动作学习隐式涌现，缺乏对交互演化趋势的显式（但非规划式）建模。这种"未来导向"信息的缺失限制了策略在长时程、需要预判的任务上的表现。作者希望在**不引入显式显式规划（explicit plan）**的前提下，为策略补充这类前瞻条件，从而提升操作成功率。

Method: 1. **核心思路**：从一段短观测历史中，让策略学习交互演化的紧凑潜变量表征（compact latent representation of interaction evolution），作为"运动趋势"的先验。
2. **训练监督**：训练阶段用**稀疏的未来夹爪状态（sparse future gripper states）**对该潜变量进行监督，使其编码未来交互走向；这种监督是稀疏的，避免了对完整轨迹的依赖。
3. **推理阶段**：只保留该潜变量，作为面向未来的条件（future-oriented conditioning）与当前观测一起输入策略，不做任何显式轨迹规划。
4. **条件注入方式**：潜变量用于**全局条件（global conditioning）**以指导动作生成；此外额外设计一个**门控 FiLM 分支（gated FiLM branch）**，仅在 UNet 的 bottleneck 处注入，实现局部、受控的特征调制。
5. **兼容性**：方法保留了原 DP3 的**密集动作（dense-action）与滚动时域（receding-horizon）**公式，属于对现有框架的最小侵入式扩展，仅增加 3.52% 参数量。

Result: 评测覆盖仿真与真机：RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-40、DexArt 以及五项真实机器人任务。主要结果（本文方法 vs. DP3）：- 50 任务 RoboTwin2.0 混合训练：**62.8% vs. 56.1%**- LIBERO-40：**71.93% vs. 37.08%**- 真实机器人五任务：**72.0% vs. 49.0%**参数开销仅增加 3.52%。结果表明，在 LIBERO-40 与真机任务上提升幅度尤为显著，说明扩散策略在"知道交互走向、但不被指定具体移动位置"的情况下可获得大幅收益。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于提出 Movement Trend Guidance：一种用稀疏未来夹爪状态监督的交互演化潜变量，作为未来导向条件注入 3D 扩散策略，在不引入显式规划、保持原有密集动作与滚动时域框架的前提下显著提升操作性能，且参数开销极低（+3.52%）。其意义在于揭示了"前瞻性条件"可作为独立于动作学习之外的有效归纳偏置，为扩散策略的时序建模提供了轻量路径。可能局限：监督信号依赖未来夹爪状态（稀疏），对更复杂/多阶段交互的适用性与潜变量的可解释性、以及在不同本体与任务分布上的泛化能力尚待进一步验证（摘要未给出消融细节）。

Abstract: 3D diffusion policies are strong at generating geometrically grounded actions from current observations, but successful manipulation requires not only knowing what motion is feasible now, but also anticipating where the interaction is heading. Existing policies largely leave such foresight to emerge implicitly from action learning. We introduce Movement Trend Guidance, a simple but effective way to provide this foresight without introducing an explicit plan. From a short observation history, the policy learns a compact latent representation of interaction evolution. During training, sparse future gripper states supervise this representation; at inference, only the latent is retained as future-oriented conditioning alongside the current observation. The latent provides global conditioning for action generation, while an additional gated FiLM branch is used only at the UNet bottleneck. Despite adding only 3.52% more parameters to DP3, our method preserves the original dense-action and receding-horizon formulation and consistently improves upon DP3 across RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-40, and DexArt. It reaches 62.8% vs. 56.1% in 50-task RoboTwin2.0 mixed training, 71.93% vs. 37.08% on LIBERO-40, and 72.0% vs. 49.0% on five real-robot tasks. These results show that a diffusion policy can benefit substantially from knowing where an interaction is heading, without being told exactly where to move.

</details>


### [206] [MAGNETAR: Multipath-Guided Spatial Posteriors for Transmitter Pose Inference in the Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20670)
*Haozhe Lei,Ruibin Chen,Yuhan Jiang,Ali Rasteh,Aditya Dhananjay,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAGNETAR 是一种从单次异步射频多径快照中联合推断发射器平面位置与朝向后验分布的方法，通过共享 2D U-Net 对离散位置-朝向网格进行条件评分与联合归一化，实现比参数化方法更优的定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱房间中定位无线电发射器时，单次测量往往与多个发射器位置甚至多个朝向（因中高频段天线具有方向性）一致，因此点估计不足。需要一种能从单次测量中推断出完整的联合后验分布（位置+朝向）的方法，以应对多径环境下的定位模糊性，这对机器人在复杂室内场景中的射频感知与导航至关重要。

Method: MAGNETAR 的输入为单次异步 RF 多径快照（由到达角 AoA 和信噪比 SNR 估计表示），同时给定房间布局和接收器位姿。方法在离散化的位置-朝向网格上推断联合后验。论文提出并比较了五种神经评分器（neural scorers），最终采用一个共享的 2D U-Net，以每个候选朝向作为条件进行评分，并对网格上的分数进行联合归一化。训练结合了经过真实-仿真标定（real-to-sim-calibrated）的 10 GHz 仿真数据和少量实测数据子集。

Result: 在留出的仿真数据集上，基于网格的联合后验优于参数化方法；在机器人实测数据上，以朝向为条件的评分器（heading-conditioned scorer）迁移表现最佳。此外，融合联合后验比融合仅含位置的边缘后验效果更好。评估围绕位置与朝向的联合后验估计精度展开。

Conclusion: MAGNETAR 的主要贡献在于：从单次 RF 多径快照中推断位置与朝向的联合后验，验证了网格化联合建模优于参数化方法，并证明朝向条件化评分器在仿真到真实迁移中的有效性，以及联合后验融合的优势。潜在局限包括：依赖于已知的房间布局和接收器位姿，仿真到真实的标定质量影响性能，且评估主要在 10 GHz 与有限实测数据下进行，泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Robots that localize a radio transmitter need more than a point estimate: in cluttered rooms, one measurement is often consistent with several transmitter locations and, because upper-mid-band antennas are directional, several headings. We present MAGNETAR, which infers a joint posterior over planar transmitter position and heading from a single asynchronous radio-frequency (RF) multipath snapshot, represented by angle-of-arrival and signal-to-noise-ratio estimates, given the room layout and receiver pose. Among our five neural scorers, MAGNETAR adopts a shared 2D U-Net conditioned on each candidate heading, jointly normalizing scores over a discretized position-heading grid. Training uses real-to-sim-calibrated 10 GHz simulations and a small measured subset. Grid-based joint posteriors outperform parametric ones on held-out simulations, the heading-conditioned scorer transfers best to robotic measurements, and fusing joint posteriors improves on fusing position-only marginals.

</details>


### [207] [Towards Scaling Marine Perception with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20680)
*Haoyu Ma,Onur Bagoren,Anja Sheppard,Elias Fandi,Ashrith Edukulla,Tanner Aslan,Natasha Sieh,Jingyu Song,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将基于 IsaacSim 的水下感知模拟器 OceanSim 扩展为一个合成数据生成（SDG）流水线，可自动生成大规模、带标注、照片级真实的水下数据集，并用于真实海水环境中的海胆检测任务，系统研究合成场景变化对仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在水下环境开展可扩展机器学习面临的核心瓶颈是缺乏真实世界的标注数据：水下数据采集成本高、过程繁琐，标注同样困难，导致大规模真实数据集难以获取和整理。合成/模拟数据有望弥合这一差距，为水下感知任务提供大量带标注训练样本。然而现有水下模拟器在渲染真实感、场景多样性以及仿真到现实迁移能力方面仍存在不足，因此需要一套可控、可扩展的合成数据生成方案，并需要系统探究何种合成场景变化真正有助于 sim-to-real 泛化。

Method: 论文在 OceanSim（基于 NVIDIA IsaacSim 的水下感知模拟器）基础上扩展出一套合成数据生成（SDG）流水线。该流水线允许用户生成大规模、自动标注、照片级真实的数据集，并支持对场景外观（appearance）、场景结构（structure）以及传感器设置（sensor settings）进行可配置化控制。作者通过控制合成场景中不同形式的变异，构建多种配置的数据集，用于训练下游水下感知模型，并据此分析各变异因素对 sim-to-real 迁移表现的影响。核心创新在于将可控的场景/传感器参数化与自动标注结合，实现面向水下感知的大规模数据合成与迁移性研究。

Result: 实验在真实世界海胆（sea urchin）检测任务上评估该流水线。研究设置围绕不同形式的合成场景变异，比较其对 sim-to-real 性能的影响，从而分析哪些外观、结构与传感器层面的变化有助于迁移到真实水下图像。论文报告并讨论了相应实验发现，同时指出当前流水线的主要局限，并对提升水下渲染真实感、场景多样性以及 sim-to-real 泛化评估方法提出了未来方向。具体量化指标（如 mAP 等）在摘要中未给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出了一个开源、可配置的水下合成数据生成流水线，支持大规模自动标注和照片级真实数据生成，并针对真实海胆检测任务开展了系统性的 sim-to-real 研究，揭示了合成场景变异对迁移性能的作用，同时坦诚讨论了当前流水线在渲染真实感、场景多样性和泛化评估方面的不足。局限在于：渲染保真度与真实水下成像仍有差距，场景多样性有限，且 sim-to-real 泛化评估体系尚不完善，这些均被作者列为未来工作方向。

Abstract: Scalable machine learning in challenging underwater environments is strongly limited by the lack of labeled real-world training data. This data is often expensive and laborious to gather, making large-scale real-world data challenging to gather and curate. However, simulated data can help close the gap, enabling many learning-based tasks for underwater perception. In this work, we extend OceanSim, an IsaacSim-based underwater perception simulator, with a Synthetic Data Generation (SDG) pipeline for training models to be used in underwater scenarios. The proposed pipeline enables users to generate large, automatically labeled, photorealistic datasets with configurable scene appearance, structure, and sensor settings. We evaluate the pipeline on a real-world sea urchin detection task and study how different forms of synthetic scene variation affect sim-to-real performance. Based on these experiments, we discuss findings on our results, main limitations of the current pipeline and identify future directions for improving underwater rendering fidelity, scene diversity, and the evaluation of sim-to-real generalization. The open-source code can be found at https://github.com/umfieldrobotics/OceanSim.

</details>


### [208] [Custom PX4 firmware for autonomous hybrid aerial-marine missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20691)
*Andrea Capuozzo,Fabio Ruggiero,Vincenzo Lippiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向混合水空两栖无人机（hybrid aerial-amphibious drone）的PX4固件扩展，在同一任务中融合飞行与水面航行，新增手动与自主海洋导航模式，并接入标准PX4任务流水线与QGroundControl界面，实现区分空中与水面航点的统一规划与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水生环境的测绘与监测需要平台既能空中快速机动、又能贴近水面长期作业。传统无人机只能飞行、无人船只能航行，单一平台难以在同一任务内兼顾两者。现有PX4生态主要面向空中飞行，缺乏对水面航行模式、混合航点与统一任务调度的支持。因此，作者希望在保留原有飞行功能与安全机制的前提下，赋予PX4水面导航能力，从而支撑水空混合任务。

Method: 核心是对PX4飞行控制固件进行扩展，而非提出新的视觉或学习模型。具体做法包括：(1) 引入手动海洋导航模式与自主海洋导航模式，与PX4既有的飞行模式和任务流水线并存；(2) 采用带类型区分的航点（differentiated aerial and marine waypoints），使同一任务中可交替编排空中与水面航段；(3) 扩展与QGroundControl的接口，实现混合任务在统一地面站下的规划、上传与监控；(4) 设计上强调保留既有飞行功能与安全机制（如失控保护、模式切换与状态机逻辑）不被破坏，从而在固件层面实现飞行与水面航行的模态统一。

Result: 论文通过仿真案例研究验证实现：在平静水面（calm）与波浪（wavy）条件下的仿真中，所提扩展能够实现稳定的水面自主导航，并可与PX4标准任务流程、QGroundControl交互正常协同执行混合航点任务。摘要未给出定量指标（如路径跟踪误差、能耗或成功率），评估以仿真功能性验证为主。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是首个（据论文描述）将水面导航模式与混合航点调度集成进PX4固件和QGroundControl的开源实践框架，使单一水空两栖平台可在同一任务内完成飞行与水面航行，服务于水生环境测绘与监测。局限在于：仅做了仿真验证，缺少真实水域的现场实验与定量性能评估；对波浪等动态干扰的鲁棒性与控制精度未量化；未涉及感知、事件相机或小型目标识别等视觉能力。

Abstract: Mapping and monitoring aquatic environments can benefit from hybrid aerial-amphibious drones able to combine flight and water-surface navigation within the same mission. This paper presents a PX4 firmware extension for such platforms, introducing manual and autonomous marine navigation modes integrated with the standard PX4 mission pipeline and QGroundControl interface. The proposed framework preserves existing flight functionalities and safety mechanisms while enabling unified planning and execution of hybrid aerial-marine missions with differentiated aerial and marine waypoints. Simulated case studies validate the implementation and demonstrate stable surface navigation under calm and wavy conditions.

</details>


### [209] [HOPHY: A Hierarchical Hypergraph Representation for Off-Road Path and Mission Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20694)
*Pranay Meshram,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Prithvi Poddar,Ehsan Tarkesh Esfahani,Chen Wang,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: \u8be5\u8bba\u6587\u63d0\u51fa HOPHY\uff08Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs\uff09\uff0c\u4e00\u79cd\u53ef\u590d\u7528\u7684\u5206\u5c42\u5730\u5f62\u8868\u793a\uff0c\u5c06\u516c\u91cc\u7ea7\u79bb\u7ebf\u5730\u56fe\u7ec4\u7ec7\u4e3a\u51e0\u4f55\u8fde\u901a\u7684\u8bed\u4e49\u533a\u57df\u3001\u4fdd\u8fde\u901a\u6027\u7684\u5173\u952e\u533a\u57df\u4ee5\u53ca\u7c7b\u578b\u5316\u8d85\u8fb9\uff0c\u5728\u4fdd\u6301\u4e0e\u50cf\u7d20 A* \u8fd1\u4f3c\u4e00\u81f4\u4ee3\u4ef7\u7684\u540c\u65f6\uff0c\u663e\u8457\u964d\u4f4e\u91cd\u590d\u67e5\u8be2\u4e0e\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u5ef6\u8fdf\uff0c\u5e76\u5728\u771f\u5b9e\u65e0\u4eba\u8f66\u5e73\u53f0\u4e0a\u5b8c\u6210 1.5 km \u516b\u4efb\u52a1\u4efb\u52a1\u3002


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \u707e\u5bb3\u54cd\u5e94\u3001\u641c\u6551\u4e0e\u6218\u672f UGV \u4efb\u52a1\u9700\u8981\u5728\u5730\u5f62\u3001\u4f53\u4e0e\u76ee\u6807\u53d8\u5316\u65f6\u53cd\u590d\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u8def\u5f84\u4e0e\u4efb\u52a1\u3002\u50cf\u7d20\u7f51\u683c\u641c\u7d22\uff08\u5982\u50cf\u7d20 A*\uff09\u5bf9\u516c\u91cc\u7ea7\u91cd\u590d\u67e5\u8be2\u4ee3\u4ef7\u9ad8\u6602\uff1b\u800c\u4f20\u7edf\u8bed\u4e49\u62bd\u8c61\u65b9\u6cd5\u5728\u73af\u5883\u53d8\u5316\u65f6\u5f88\u96be\u7ef4\u6301\u6709\u6548\u4ee3\u4ef7\u4e0e\u8fde\u901a\u6027\u3002\u56e0\u6b64\uff0c\u5982\u4f55\u6784\u5efa\u4e00\u79cd\u53ef\u590d\u7528\u3001\u53ef\u5c40\u90e8\u66f4\u65b0\u7684\u9ad8\u6548\u5206\u5c42\u5730\u5f62\u8868\u793a\uff0c\u5728\u4fdd\u8bc1\u89c4\u5212\u8d28\u91cf\u7684\u524d\u63d0\u4e0b\u5927\u5e45\u964d\u4f4e\u91cd\u590d\u67e5\u8be2\u4e0e\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u6210\u672c\uff0c\u662f\u63d0\u5347\u4efb\u52a1\u7ea7\u81ea\u4e3b\u6027\u7684\u5173\u952e\u3002

Method: HOPHY \u4ee5\u8d85\u56fe\uff08hypergraph\uff09\u4e3a\u6838\u5fc3\u6784\u5efa\u4e09\u5c42\u5206\u5c42\u5730\u5f62\u8868\u793a\uff1a1\uff09GSNodes\uff0c\u5c06\u5730\u56fe\u5c3a\u5ea6\u5730\u5f62\u7ec4\u7ec7\u4e3a\u51e0\u4f55\u8fde\u901a\u7684\u8bed\u4e49\u533a\u57df\uff1b2\uff09Coarse Regions\uff0c\u4fdd\u6301\u8fde\u901a\u6027\u7684\u5173\u952e\u533a\u57df\uff1b3\uff09\u7c7b\u578b\u5316\u8d85\u8fb9\uff0c\u7f16\u7801\u5730\u5f62\u3001\u4f53\u4e0e\u5929\u6c14\u4e0a\u4e0b\u6587\u3002\u5173\u952e\u521b\u65b0\u5728\u4e8e\u5229\u7528\u8d85\u8fb9\u4ea4\u96c6\uff08hyperedge intersections\uff09\u5b9a\u4f4d\u53d7\u5f71\u54cd\u533a\u57df\u4e0e\u76f8\u5173\u8fb9\uff0c\u4ece\u800c\u5728\u72b6\u6001\u66f4\u65b0\u65f6\u65e0\u9700\u91cd\u5efa\u6574\u4e2a\u5c42\u6b21\u7ed3\u6784\uff0c\u5b9e\u73b0\u589e\u91cf\u5f0f\u5730\u56fe\u7ef4\u62a4\u4e0e\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u3002

Result: \u5728\u8de8\u8d8a\u516c\u91cc\u7ea7\u9762\u79ef\u7684\u771f\u5b9e\u975e\u94fa\u88c5\u8def\u9762\u5730\u56fe\u4e0a\uff0cHOPHY \u5b9e\u73b0\u4e86 100% \u89c4\u5212\u6210\u529f\u7387\uff0c\u4e0e\u9884\u8a00\u5668 oracle\uff08\u50cf\u7d20 A*\uff09\u76f8\u6bd4\u4e2d\u4f4d\u4ee3\u4ef7\u504f\u5dee\u4f4e\u4e8e 0.01%\uff0c\u4e14\u67e5\u8be2\u4e0e\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u5ef6\u8fdf\u663e\u8457\u4f4e\u4e8e\u6240\u5bf9\u6bd4\u7684\u50cf\u7d20\u4e0e\u62bd\u8c61\u57fa\u7ebf\u3002\u5728\u591a\u673a\u5668\u4eba\u4efb\u52a1\u5206\u914d\uff08MRTA\uff09\u95ee\u9898\u4e2d\uff0c\u76f8\u6bd4\u50cf\u7d20 A* \u51cf\u5c11\u603b\u8ba1\u7b97\u91cf 79 \u500d\uff0c\u76f8\u6bd4\u6700\u5feb\u7684\u62bd\u8c61\u57fa\u7ebf\u51cf\u5c11 7.2 \u500d\uff0c\u4efb\u52a1\u603b\u5de5\u671f\uff08makespan\uff09\u4e0e\u50cf\u7d20 A* \u76f8\u5f53\u3002\u6700\u540e\u5728\u5b9e\u4f53 Clearpath Jackal \u65e0\u4eba\u8f66\u4e0a\u9a8c\u8bc1\uff0c\u5b8c\u6210\u4e86\u8de8\u6df7\u5408\u8def\u9762\u6237\u5916\u5730\u5f62\u7684 1.5 km\u3001\u516b\u4efb\u52a1\u4efb\u52a1\uff0c\u5e76\u5728\u906d\u9047\u963b\u585e\u540e\u6210\u529f\u6267\u884c\u89e6\u53d1\u5f0f\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u8def\u7ebf\u3002

Conclusion: HOPHY \u8d21\u732e\u4e86\u4e00\u79cd\u53ef\u590d\u7528\u3001\u652f\u6301\u589e\u91cf\u66f4\u65b0\u7684\u8d85\u56fe\u5206\u5c42\u5730\u5f62\u8868\u793a\uff0c\u5728\u4fdd\u8bc1\u4e0e\u50cf\u7d20\u7ea7\u89c4\u5212\u51e0\u4e4e\u7b49\u4ef7\u7684\u4ee3\u4ef7\u4e0b\uff0c\u663e\u8457\u964d\u4f4e\u4e86\u516c\u91cc\u7ea7\u91cd\u590d\u67e5\u8be2\u4e0e\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u7684\u8ba1\u7b97\u8d1f\u62c5\uff0c\u5e76\u5728\u591a\u673a\u5668\u4eba\u4efb\u52a1\u5206\u914d\u4e0e\u5b9e\u673a\u90e8\u7f72\u573a\u666f\u4e2d\u5f97\u5230\u9a8c\u8bc1\u3002\u5176\u610f\u4e49\u5728\u4e8e\u4e3a\u707e\u5bb3\u54cd\u5e94\u3001\u641c\u6551\u7b49\u9700\u8981\u53cd\u590d\u91cd\u89c4\u5212\u7684\u4efb\u52a1\u7ea7\u81ea\u4e3b\u6027\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u9ad8\u6548\u7684\u5730\u56fe\u62bd\u8c61\u65b9\u6848\u3002\u6f5c\u5728\u5c40\u9650\u5305\u62ec\uff1a\u8d85\u56fe\u6784\u5efa\u4e0e\u8bed\u4e49\u533a\u57df\u63d0\u53d6\u4f9d\u8d56\u7279\u5b9a\u5730\u5f62\u4e0e\u4f20\u611f\u914d\u7f6e\uff0c\u56e0\u679c\u8def\u9762\u4e0e\u52a8\u6001\u969c\u788d\u7684\u6cdb\u5316\u6027\u3001\u6784\u5efa\u9636\u6bb5\u5f00\u9500\u4e0e\u8de8\u533a\u57df\u8fc1\u79fb\u80fd\u529b\u5c1a\u9700\u8fdb\u4e00\u6b65\u9a8c\u8bc1\u3002

Abstract: Mission-level autonomy for disaster response, search and rescue, and tactical UGV operations requires repeated path and mission planning as terrain conditions, agent types, and objectives change. Pixel-grid search is costly for repeated kilometer-scale queries, while semantic abstractions must maintain valid costs and connectivity as conditions change. We present HOPHY (Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs), a reusable hierarchical terrain representation that organizes map-scale terrain into geometrically connected semantic regions (GSNodes), connectivity-preserving critical regions (Coarse Regions), and typed hyperedges for terrain, agent, and weather context. Hyperedge intersections select affected regions and incident edges for state updates without rebuilding the hierarchy. Across real off-road maps spanning kilometer-scale areas, HOPHY achieves 100% planning success and less than 0.01% median cost deviation from the oracle (pixel A*), with substantially lower query and replanning latency than the evaluated pixel and abstraction baselines. Applied to a multi-robot task-allocation (MRTA) problem, these gains reduce total computation by 79x over pixel A* and 7.2x over the fastest abstraction baseline, with mission makespan comparable to pixel A*. Finally, we demonstrate HOPHY on a physical Clearpath Jackal that successfully executes a 1.5-km, eight-task mission across mixed-surface outdoor terrain and a blockage-triggered replanned route.

</details>


### [210] [MoWAM: Explicit Future Motion Prediction for Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20709)
*Jiayu Wang,Bin Zhu,Yue Yu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoWAM 提出一种高效的世界动作模型（World Action Model），用「显式的未来运动预测」替代「未来视频生成」，在训练时联合学习视觉动力学、运动预测与动作生成，推理时完全去除视频生成，从而在保持对未来的显式建模的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过引入未来动力学来提升机器人策略学习，但传统方法需要在推理阶段显式生成未来视频，带来巨大的计算开销。若直接去掉未来生成，虽然提升了效率，却使未来动力学只能隐式地编码在观测特征中，导致模型在分布偏移（distribution shift）下鲁棒性下降。因此，核心问题是如何在避免昂贵的未来视频生成的同时，仍保留对未来的显式表示，以兼顾效率与鲁棒性。

Method: MoWAM 的核心思想是用「结构化的机器人运动」作为未来的紧凑抽象，而非重建完整的未来场景。具体而言：1）不生成完整未来画面，而是预测机器人未来如何随当前场景与交互约束演化；2）采用 Mixture-of-Transformer 架构，在训练阶段学习未来视觉动力学，并联合预测运动（motion）与动作（action）；3）推理时彻底移除视频生成路径，但仍保留显式的未来表示（运动抽象）；4）利用紧凑的运动表示实现高效的推理期扩展（inference-time scaling）：通过采样多组「运动-动作」候选对，并使用一个运动感知的任务进度验证器（motion-aware task-progress verifier）进行筛选。

Result: 论文在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 以及真实世界操作任务上进行实验。结果显示，MoWAM 在分布内（in-distribution）性能上表现强劲，在分布外（out-of-distribution）鲁棒性上有所提升，并在真实世界任务中取得高于代表性 WAM 基线的平均成功率。此外，随着候选样本数量的增加，性能持续提升，表明显式未来运动为推理期扩展提供了有效且高效的依据。

Conclusion: MoWAM 的主要贡献在于：提出以「未来运动预测」取代「未来视频生成」的高效世界动作模型范式，通过 Mixture-of-Transformer 在训练时联合建模视觉动力学、运动与动作，并在推理时免除视频生成；同时借助紧凑运动表示实现基于候选采样与运动感知验证器的推理期扩展。其意义在于兼顾了效率、显式未来建模与鲁棒性。可能的局限包括：运动抽象的表达能力是否足以覆盖复杂/非结构化场景、验证器对任务进度的判断可靠性，以及推理期扩展带来的采样成本与收益权衡等（论文摘要未给出具体数值与失效情况的量化分析）。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by incorporating future dynamics, yet explicitly generating future videos at inference introduces substantial computational overhead. Removing future generation improves efficiency, but leaves future dynamics only implicitly encoded in observation features, which can limit robustness under distribution shifts. We propose MoWAM, an efficient WAM that replaces future video generation with explicit future motion prediction. Instead of reconstructing the complete future scene, MoWAM models structured robot motion as a compact abstraction of the future, capturing how the robot is expected to evolve under the current scene and interaction constraints. A Mixture-of-Transformer architecture learns future visual dynamics during training while jointly predicting motion and action, allowing video generation to be removed entirely at inference while retaining an explicit representation of the future. The compact motion representation further enables efficient inference-time scaling by sampling multiple candidates of motion and action pairs and selecting among them with a motion-aware task-progress verifier. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that MoWAM achieves strong in-distribution performance, improved out-of-distribution robustness, and higher average real-world success than representative WAM baselines. In addition, performance improves as more candidates are explored, demonstrating that explicit future motion provides an effective and efficient basis for inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [211] [Underwater Visual Target Tracking with Target-Specific Depth Estimation and Adaptive Model-Fusion Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20731)
*Yuheng Zhou,Haiyang Cheng,Yanqi Feng,Pangkit Fong,Mei Xuan Lee,Marcus Gee,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向自主水下航行器（AUV）的立体视觉伺服框架，用于水下目标跟踪。感知端通过目标深度掩膜与卡尔曼滤波从立体图像中提取稳定3D相对状态；控制端将偏航调节与平移控制解耦，并采用自适应模型融合的平移MPC，在未知目标运动下实现实时跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下目标跟踪对AUV自主作业至关重要，但面临两大难题：一是水下场景中深度测量不可靠（颜色失真、纹理退化、视差噪声），导致3D相对状态估计不稳定；二是目标运动模式未知（可能匀速、静止或切换），传统单一运动模型难以适应。同时，多自由度（multi-DOF）耦合优化计算代价高，难以满足实时控制需求。因此需要一种既能获得鲁棒深度感知、又能实时生成可靠控制指令的视觉伺服方案。

Method: 框架分为感知与控制两部分。感知：从立体图像中通过目标专属深度提取获得3D相对状态，构建结合颜色、视差与时间线索的目标深度掩膜以筛选可靠目标像素，并对深度测量与检测到的图像中心分别进行卡尔曼滤波，抑制噪声。控制：将偏航调节与平移控制解耦，避免计算昂贵的耦合多自由度优化，从而支持实时的平移模型预测控制（MPC）。平移控制器采用自适应模型融合预测控制，融合恒速（constant-velocity）与零速（zero-velocity）两种目标运动模型，以适配不同目标运动模式；模型权重根据历史预测误差在线更新，并在执行器能力、跟随距离与视场角（FOV）约束下计算平移控制指令。

Result: 论文通过仿真与真实世界实验进行验证，证明该框架的有效性，并表明其性能优于现有框架。评估聚焦于水下目标跟踪任务，涉及深度估计稳定性与实时控制性能。（摘要未给出具体数据集、指标数值与消融细节。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：以目标深度掩膜加卡尔曼滤波的感知方案提升水下深度鲁棒性，并以偏航-平移解耦加自适应模型融合MPC的控制方案实现实时、适应未知目标运动的AUV视觉伺服，仿真与实机实验均验证其优越性。潜在局限包括：摘要未提供定量指标与失败案例分析，模型融合仅含恒速与零速两类模型，对突发机动或强扰动目标泛化能力、以及对不同水下环境的光照与浊度鲁棒性仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Vision-based underwater target tracking is challenged by unreliable depth measurements and unknown target motion. This paper proposes a stereo visual-servoing framework for an autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV). For perception, the framework derives a stable 3D relative state from stereo images through target-specific depth extraction and Kalman filtering. It constructs a target-depth mask from color, disparity, and temporal cues to select reliable target pixels, and then filters the resulting depth measurement and detected image center separately. For control, the framework decouples yaw regulation from translational control, avoiding computationally expensive coupled multi-DOF optimization and enabling real-time translational MPC. The translational controller employs adaptive model-fusion predictive control, combining constant-velocity and zero-velocity target models to accommodate different target-motion patterns. It updates the model weights using historical prediction errors and computes translational commands subject to actuation, following-distance, and field-of-view constraints. Through simulations and real-world experiments, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and show it has better performance than existing frameworks.

</details>


### [212] [MILER: Semantic Mid-Level Representation for Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning in Unstructured Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20747)
*Thomas Steinecker,Denis Trescher,Alexander Bienemann,Thorsten Luettel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILER 是一个面向非结构化环境的端到端自主驾驶策略框架，通过"语义中层表征（MLR）+ 轨迹对齐"实现感知与控制的双重零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，并在真实车辆上完成 17.3 km 无人干预驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）具备超越人类表现与自学习策略的潜力，但在真实世界自主驾驶（尤其非结构化环境）中应用极少，核心障碍是 sim-to-real 迁移困难：非结构化环境缺乏规则道路结构，感知输入分布与仿真差异大，且仿真中训练出的控制动作难以直接作用于真实车辆动力学。因此作者希望构建一个既能复用仿真中 RL 训练的控制策略、又能在真实车上零样本部署的完整软件栈。

Method: 提出 MILER 端到端策略框架，分训练与部署两阶段。离线训练阶段：设计自定义的语义中层表征（Mid-Level Representation, MLR）模拟器，训练 RL 策略网络，其控制输出直接作用于自行车模型（bicycle model）动力学。部署阶段：真实车辆的相机与 LiDAR 数据经 BEVFusion 融合，生成与 MLR 模拟器一致的语义鸟瞰图（BEV）表征，从而对齐感知域。关键创新在于动作不直接施加于实车，而是采用轨迹对齐（trajectory-alignment）策略，将策略网络输出的动作转化为可行轨迹，实现感知与控制两方面的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。整套软件栈运行于 Jetson AGX Orin 边缘平台。

Result: 在一条包含多种障碍物、发夹弯、最高 33.6 km/h 速度以及越野路段的 3.0 km 多样化测试赛道上进行评估。使用两辆不同车辆，在无人工干预下共行驶 17.3 km，验证了框架的有效性与鲁棒性。全部软件栈在 Jetson AGX Orin 上实时运行，体现了边缘部署可行性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：提出统一的 MLR 语义中层表征桥接仿真与真实感知；通过轨迹对齐策略实现感知+控制的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移；在真实非结构化环境中完成长距离无人驾驶验证并实现边缘端运行。意义在于为非结构化环境下 RL 自主驾驶的落地提供了可复用范式。潜在局限包括：MLR 模拟器与 BEVFusion 的表征一致性依赖人工设计，泛化到更复杂/动态场景（如其他交通参与者交互、恶劣天气）的能力尚不明确，且未报告定量指标（如成功率、碰撞率、与基线对比）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning constitutes a promising approach owing to its potential for superhuman performance and self-learned policies. However, its application to real-world autonomous driving remains scarce, particularly in unstructured environments, because of the challenges associated with sim-to-real transfer for unstructured environments. In this work, we present MILER, an end-to-end policy framework with zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. During offline training, we employ a custom semantic mid-level representation (MLR) simulator and train the policy network using reinforcement learning, with its control outputs applied directly to a bicycle model. During deployment on the real vehicle, camera and LiDAR data are processed by BEVFusion to generate a semantic bird's-eye-view representation consistent with that of the MLR simulator. The actions generated by the policy network are not applied directly to the real vehicle. Instead, we employ a trajectory-alignment strategy that enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of both perception and control. We extensively evaluate the proposed framework on a diverse test track comprising numerous challenges, including various obstacles, hairpin curves, velocities of up to 33.6 km/h, and off-road sections. In total, we drove 17.3 km with two different vehicles on a 3.0 km test track without human intervention, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, the entire software stack runs on a Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [213] [OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756)
*Damiano Da Col,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Kashyap Chitta,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OPTED，一种用于端到端自动驾驶策略的闭环后训练框架。其核心思想是将强化学习(RL)与端到端策略后训练解耦：先用RL在特权向量化输入（HD地图+边界框）上训练一个教师策略，再由该教师为预训练的相机学生模型在闭环仿真中提供监督信号。在 AlpaSim（基于3DGS神经重建真实驾驶日志）中对 TransFuser 和 VaVAM 两个相机模型微调，驾驶分数分别提升 1.6× 和 9.5×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠扩大预训练数据带来的收益递减，后训练在自动驾驶等物理AI领域愈发重要。端到端驾驶策略通常以行为克隆方式在人类示范上进行开环预训练，但闭环部署时误差累积会使车辆偏离训练数据分布，增加安全关键事故风险。闭环后训练可缓解该问题，然而对基于传感器的策略而言，直接闭环RL需要昂贵的仿真交互与奖励设计，成本高且可能导致策略偏离人类先验。

Method: OPTED 采用师生两阶段解耦设计。(1) 特权教师阶段：在低维向量化输入（HD地图与边界框）上用RL训练教师策略，输入表示紧凑、便于高效探索与奖励优化。(2) 学生后训练阶段：将预训练的相机端到端策略放入闭环仿真中，由特权教师在相同场景下提供监督（动作/轨迹层面的引导），从而在闭环分布上微调学生，而非直接对其做RL。这一解耦使学生只在视觉观测上运行，避免了对感知模块做RL的高昂采样成本，同时通过教师引导保持接近人类先验的行为。方法被应用于 TransFuser（基于Transformer的传感器融合规划）与 VaVAM（视觉-动作模型）两种相机模型。

Result: 实验在 AlpaSim 中进行，使用对真实驾驶日志的神经重建（3DGS）构建闭环仿真环境。评估采用驾驶分数作为主要指标。结果显示：对 TransFuser 和 VaVAM 分别实现 1.6× 与 9.5× 的驾驶分数提升。对照实验表明，OPTED 在达到与直接RL后训练相当的闭环性能时，所需的仿真器交互量约少三个数量级，同时策略与人类先验的偏离更小。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出将RL与端到端策略后训练解耦的OPTED框架，用特权向量化教师监督相机学生，在显著降低仿真交互成本的同时提升闭环驾驶性能并保持人类先验一致性。其意义在于为传感器端到端策略提供了一种可扩展的闭环后训练范式，并借助3DGS神经重建降低对真实闭环数据的需求。潜在局限包括：依赖特权信息与HD地图/边界框标注来训练教师、对3DGS重建仿真的真实性与覆盖度敏感，以及仅在两个相机模型和单一仿真平台（AlpaSim）上验证，向真实世界及其他感知模态的泛化性尚待进一步检验。

Abstract: As scaling pre-training data alone yields diminishing returns, post-training is becoming increasingly important across physical AI domains such as autonomous driving. End-to-end driving policies are pre-trained in open loop with behavior cloning on human demonstrations. However, compounding errors during closed-loop deployment can take the vehicle outside the training data distribution, increasing the risk of safety-critical incidents. Closed-loop post-training can mitigate this risk but requires costly simulation for sensor-based policies. We propose OPTED (on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving) which decouples reinforcement learning from the post-training of the end-to-end policy: a privileged teacher is trained using RL on vectorized inputs (HD-map and bounding boxes). This teacher then provides supervision to the pre-trained student during closed-loop post-training. We apply OPTED to two camera-based models, TransFuser and VaVAM, and fine-tune them in AlpaSim, using neural reconstructions (3DGS) of real driving logs. Driving scores increase by factors of 1.6$\times$ and 9.5$\times$, respectively. In controlled experiments OPTED matches closed-loop performance with approximately three orders of magnitude fewer simulator interactions than direct RL post-training, while staying closer to the human prior. Project page: https://01dami23.github.io/opted/

</details>


### [214] [Agile-WAM: An Agile Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20761)
*Hanchu Zhou,Brendan Lynch,Raman Goyal,Dechen Gao,Begum Kasap,Boqi Zhao,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TARO，一个敏捷的触觉世界动作模型（Tactile World Action Model），通过共享潜空间与直接视觉-触觉到动作的流匹配（flow-matching）过程，联合生成动作块及未来视觉/触觉潜表示，可在 11.9 ms 推理延迟下实现接触丰富的机器人精细控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉运动策略（visuomotor policy）仅从观测映射到动作，难以学习物理动力学；世界动作模型（WAM）通过联合预测未来世界状态与动作来弥补这一缺陷。然而现有触觉 WAM 多依赖大规模预训练生成式骨干网络来建模接触丰富的物理动力学，导致推理效率低、部署不灵活，难以满足高频、精确的机器人控制需求。因此需要一个既能保留 WAM 的动力学建模能力、又具备敏捷架构与低延迟的触觉世界动作模型。

Method: TARO 将视觉与触觉观测编码到一个共享潜空间，并以此为起点执行直接的"视觉-触觉到动作"流匹配过程，在一次过程中联合生成动作块潜表示以及未来的视觉/触觉潜表示。关键设计是"多时间尺度（multi-horizon）多模态预测"：鉴于相邻视觉帧高度相似、而触觉信号在接触瞬间会突变，TARO 对视觉潜表示采用较大时间偏移的监督，而对触觉潜表示则预测下一帧，以捕捉细粒度的接触动力学。该设计使模型无需依赖大规模生成式预训练骨干即可建模接触物理，从而实现轻量、低延迟的推理。

Result: 在 9 个仿真任务与 5 个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上进行评估。TARO 在成功率上优于最强基线，同时保持低推理延迟。在 5 项真实世界实验中，整体成功率相对提升 29.4%，推理延迟为 11.9 ms。结果表明，多模态 WAM 可由敏捷架构实现，适用于精确且高频的机器人控制。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出了一种不依赖大规模生成式骨干的敏捷触觉世界动作模型，通过共享潜空间、视觉-触觉到动作流匹配以及面向不同时间尺度的多模态多尺度预测，兼顾了接触动力学建模与实时性。其意义在于展示了 WAM 在精细、高频机器人控制中的可行性。局限方面，论文未给出详细的消融与失败案例分析，方法主要在接触丰富操作场景中验证，是否可泛化至更广泛的视觉/触觉任务与真实部署环境仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) advance beyond conventional visuomotor policies by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions, enabling the policy to learn physical dynamics that support effective control. However, recent tactile WAMs often rely on large-scale pretrained generative backbones to capture contact-rich physical dynamics, which limit their inference efficiency and flexible deployment. In this paper, we present \ABBR{}, an agile tactile World Action Model for contact-rich robot control. \ABBR{} encodes visual and tactile observations into a shared latent that serves as the source of a direct vision-tactile-to-action flow-matching process, which can jointly generate latent representations of action chunks and future visual/tactile latents. A key observation is that vision and tactile signals evolve at inherently different timescales: adjacent visual frames are often highly similar, whereas tactile signals can change abruptly upon contact. We therefore introduce multi-horizon multimodal prediction in \ABBR{}, which provides supervision for visual latent at a larger temporal offset while predicting the tactile latent in the next frame to capture fine-grained contact dynamics. Across nine simulated and five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, \ABBR{} demonstrates strong and robust performance, outperforming the strongest baseline in success rate while maintaining low inference latency. In particular, in five real-world experiments, \ABBR{} yields a relative gain of $\textbf{29.4\%}$ in overall success rates while achieving inference latency of $\textbf{11.9 ms}$. These results demonstrate that multimodal WAM can be achieved with an agile architecture suitable for precise and high-frequency robot control. More details are available on our project page: https://hanchuzhou.github.io/TARO_project_page/.

</details>


### [215] [Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20822)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Zhen Dong,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文首次系统评估了"编码智能体"（coding agent）在机器人操作中的安全性问题，指出其虽能完成操作任务却忽视安全约束、频繁碰撞障碍物，并提出 SafeHarness——通过障碍感知的路径规划与接触执行两个"约束套件"，让模型在规划层面优先满足安全约束，取得 71.9% 任务成功率与 87.5% 避障率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体范式（由语言模型编写机器人控制程序，无需机器人特定训练即可操作机器人）已被证明可有效完成操作任务，但其安全性从未被考察。作者提出问题设定：每个操作任务同时配有一个机器人不可触碰的障碍物。实验发现，智能体在大多数情况下会撞上障碍物——它把任务完成当作唯一目标，完全忽视安全约束。作者进一步指出故障根源：智能体在推理轨迹（trace）中确实"看到并推理"了障碍物，提示词也明确禁止触碰，因此问题既不在感知也不在指令遵循，而在规划阶段——安全约束从未被转化为优先级。这一问题重要，因为编码智能体正被推向真实机器人部署，安全是前提条件。

Method: 作者将操作分解为"路径阶段（route phase）"和"富接触时刻（contact-rich moment）"以定位失败来源。路径阶段：模型无法为安全约束设置优先级，既没有"可通行路径（clearing route）"的概念，也不会在所选路径变得不可行时重新规划；接触时刻：模型没有意识到接触执行同样受该约束限制。为此提出 SafeHarness，为模型配备两个障碍感知"套件（harness）"：(1) 障碍感知路径规划（Obstacle-aware route planning）：将物体 grounded 为边界框，并在其上以航点（waypoint）序列形式绘制候选路径；智能体先规划路径、再验证、必要时重新规划，最后才执行。(2) 障碍感知接触执行（Obstacle-aware contact execution）：在接触阶段选择接触位置时，使接触本身避开障碍物。二者共同把安全约束注入到规划的优先层级中，而非仅靠提示词约束。

Result: 在"操作目标 + 不可触碰障碍物"的安全约束任务设定下进行评估。SafeHarness 达到 71.9% 任务成功率与 87.5% 避障率，相比此前 SOTA 分别提升 6.5% 与 27.0%；这两个数值分别是同一智能体在不使用 harness 时的 2.3 倍与 1.5 倍，表明避障能力的提升幅度显著大于任务完成能力的提升，说明 harness 主要弥补的是安全约束未被优先化的缺陷。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：首次对编码智能体进行安全性评估，指出其失败源于规划阶段而非感知或指令遵循；通过"路径—接触"分解定位故障来源；提出 SafeHarness 这一障碍感知双套件框架，以边界框 grounding、航点路径预规划与验证重规划、接触位置选择等机制将安全约束提升为规划优先级，显著提升任务成功率与避障率。意义在于为无机器人特定训练的编码智能体范式补足安全短板，推动其向真实部署靠拢。潜在局限：方法仍依赖语言模型自身的几何/空间推理能力与边界框表征精度；安全机制以显式障碍物为对象，对动态障碍、复杂接触力约束、更密集杂乱环境的泛化性尚不明确；评测集中于特定任务与障碍设定，跨平台、跨任务的安全性验证有待扩展。

Abstract: Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific training.Whether this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in its traces, and the prompt already forbids touching it, so neither perception nor instruction is at fault; the fault lies in the planning, where the stated constraint never becomes a priority. By decomposing manipulation into a route phase and a contact-rich moment, we locate the source of the failure. Along the route, the model cannot prioritize the safety constraint, having no notion of a clearing route and none of replanning once a chosen route becomes infeasible. At the contact, it is unaware that contact execution is bounded by the same constraint. To close this gap, we present SafeHarness, which equips the model with two obstacle-aware harnesses that enable it to prioritize the safety constraint. Obstacle-aware route planning grounds the objects as bounding boxes and draws candidate routes over them as sequences of waypoints. The agent then plans a route in advance, verifies it, replans when necessary, and only then executes it. Obstacle-aware contact execution instead selects the contact position so that the contact itself avoids the obstacle. SafeHarness attains 71.9% task success and 87.5% collision avoidance, surpassing the previous SOTA by 6.5% and 27.0%, respectively. These results are $2.3\times$ and $1.5\times$ those of the same agent without harnesses.

</details>


### [216] [GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20776)
*Xin Chen,Sen Chen,Yujuan Ding,Jian Liu,Guoqing Wang,Wei Ye,Heng Tao Shen,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoAAC 提出一种基于几何的自适应动作分块（action chunking）方法，用于流匹配（Flow Matching）式的 VLA 策略。它利用去噪轨迹的几何变化来刻画动作前缀的预测可靠性，从而在单次生成中自适应地确定动作视界（horizon），无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块是 VLA 策略中动作生成与执行的常用范式，但现有方法普遍采用固定动作视界。在单次 rollout 过程中，不同任务阶段对动作连续性、控制精度和闭环反馈的需求不同，固定视界难以适配动态变化的控制需求，导致在需要精细控制或频繁反馈时表现受限。该问题对于真实机器人操作（如长时序、多阶段任务）尤为关键。

Method: GeoAAC 针对流匹配（Flow Matching）生成的 VLA 策略：观察到 Flow Matching 去噪轨迹的几何特性可提供过程级信息，且动作前缀间的几何变化与预测不确定性呈正相关。基于此，方法利用前缀级（prefix-wise）几何信息构建视界级几何剖面（horizon-wise geometric profile），据此从单次生成中自适应确定动作视界，无需额外训练或迭代采样。该方法被集成到 GR00T N1.5 与 π0.5 等流式 VLA 策略中。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365 仿真基准及真实世界操作任务上，使用 GR00T N1.5 和 π0.5 进行实验。相比固定动作视界基线及现有自适应方法，GeoAAC 取得一致提升：仿真中最高提升 8.7 个百分点，真实世界平均成功率从 53.3% 提升至 74.4%。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种免训练、基于去噪几何的自适应动作分块机制，将流匹配生成过程的内在几何信息用于可靠性估计与视界决策，验证了其在不同 VLA 骨干和仿真/真实任务上的有效性。潜在局限在于方法依赖 Flow Matching 类策略的几何性质，对其他生成式策略（如扩散或自回归）适用性尚待验证，且几何-不确定性的相关性可能受任务分布影响。

Abstract: Action chunking is widely used for action generation and execution in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, yet existing approaches commonly use a fixed action horizon. During a rollout, different task stages may require different levels of action continuity, control precision, and closed-loop feedback, making a fixed horizon unable to accommodate changing control requirements. We propose \textbf{GeoAAC}, a geometry-based adaptive action chunking method for flow-based VLA policies that adjusts the action horizon according to the reliability of the current action prediction. We show that the geometry of Flow Matching denoising trajectories provides process-level information for characterizing prediction reliability, with geometric variation across action prefixes remaining positively correlated with predictive uncertainty. GeoAAC uses this prefix-wise geometry to construct a horizon-wise geometric profile and adaptively determine the action horizon from a single generation without additional training. Experiments with GR00T N1.5 and π0.5 on LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa365, and real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements over fixed-action-horizon baselines and existing adaptive methods, including up to 8.7 percentage points in simulation and an increase in average real-world success rate from 53.3\% to 74.4\%.

</details>


### [217] [StageGuard: Learning Stage Transitions for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks via Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20791)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Yangzheng Wu,Tengyue Ba,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StageGuard 是一个"智能体蒸馏"框架，用于分层机器人控制中的阶段切换（skill termination / subtask advancement）决策：借助大模型教师推理与示范轨迹生成结构化解释，蒸馏到轻量学生 VLM，用于准确且低延迟的在线阶段转换预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层规划框架需要组合多个机器人控制策略（skills）以完成长时程任务，其中"何时终止当前技能并进入下一子任务"是关键决策点。现有方法依赖预先设计的完成信号检查器（completion signal checkers），在真实执行中难以获得/设计。大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽具备强推理能力，但其决策边界与任务完成判据并不天然对齐；同时云端部署与冗长推理带来显著延迟，无法满足实时监控需求。因此需要既准确又高效的阶段转换判定方案。

Method: 提出 StageGuard 智能体蒸馏框架，包含教师模型推理与学生模型蒸馏两阶段：1）教师侧：结合教师模型推理能力与示范轨迹（demonstration trajectories），生成对子任务完成情况与策略切换的结构化解释（structured explanations）；2）学生侧：轻量级学生 VLM 基于这些解释生成紧凑的自解释（compact self-explanations），并以监督微调（SFT）方式学习。最终学生模型以紧凑自解释为输出/依据，实现在线、低算力的阶段切换预测，从而缓解云端 VLM 的延迟问题并使决策边界与任务完成判据对齐。

Result: 在两个基准的轨迹数据上评估阶段转换预测性能；并通过集成到分层机器人控制中，在 BEHAVIOR-1K 上考察闭环任务成功率，同时在真实机器人上做进一步验证。结果报告了阶段转换预测性能的显著提升，并支持高效的在线监控（低延迟）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出智能体蒸馏框架 StageGuard，将大模型教师的结构化推理知识迁移到轻量学生 VLM，用于分层机器人控制中的阶段切换决策，在预测精度与在线效率之间取得平衡，并获得仿真（BEHAVIOR-1K 闭环）与真实机器人验证。潜在局限：摘要未给出具体数据集规模、指标数值与量化延迟，泛化性依赖教师解释与示范轨迹质量，蒸馏过程可能继承教师推理中的偏差。

Abstract: Hierarchical planning frameworks combine skills from multiple robot control policies for long-horizon task execution, where determining when to terminate the current skill and advance to the next subtask is essential. Existing approaches often rely on pre-designed completion signal checkers that are hard to obtain in real-world execution. Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, but their decision boundaries are not inherently aligned with task completion criteria, while cloud deployment and lengthy reasoning introduce substantial latency, limiting real-time monitoring. We propose StageGuard, an agentic distillation framework for accurate and efficient stage-transition decisions. StageGuard combines teacher-model reasoning with demonstration trajectories to generate structured explanations of subtask completion and policy switching. A lightweight student VLM uses these explanations to generate compact self-explanations, which are used for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate stage-transition prediction on trajectories from two benchmarks and assess closed-loop task success through integration into hierarchical robot control on BEHAVIOR-1K, with further validation on real robots. Results show substantial improvements in stage-transition prediction while supporting efficient online monitoring.

</details>


### [218] [Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20820)
*Nitish Dashora,Douglas Chen,Idan Shenfeld,John Marangola,Pulkit Agrawal,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 workspace token——一种轻量级潜在记忆表征，用于长时程机器人操作任务。其核心思想是将昂贵的 VLM 推理从部署阶段前移到训练阶段：训练时用 VLM 识别完成任务所需的关键历史与当前信息，并通过集合重建解码器损失蒸馏进一个紧凑的潜在 token；部署时该 token 可直接替代观测输入，无需在推理循环中调用 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的机器人操作任务通常需要长时程的过去事件与动作记忆。然而直接以完整历史作为策略条件会引入虚假相关（spurious correlations）并导致性能退化。现有做法是在推理循环内（in-the-loop）反复进行昂贵的 VLM 查询，以在线筛选出任务相关的显著信息，但这种方式计算开销大、延迟高，难以满足实时部署需求。因此，如何在不牺牲记忆能力的前提下获得高效、可实时查询的记忆表征，是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 作者提出 workspace token 作为历史信息的压缩潜在表征，采用两阶段训练流程：(1) 利用 VLM 识别完成当前任务所必需的当前信息与历史信息；(2) 通过一个集合重建解码器损失（set-reconstruction decoder loss）将 VLM 筛选出的信息蒸馏进 workspace token 中。该 token 被设计为观测（observations）的即插即用替代品（drop-in replacement），从而在部署阶段使策略能够在完全不进行 VLM 推理的情况下完成记忆密集型任务。架构上属于"潜在记忆 + 策略条件化"范式，将多模态大模型的语义筛选能力离线固化到轻量 token 中。

Result: 论文在仿真环境与真实硬件（hardware）两类平台上进行了验证，将 workspace token 作为观测的替代输入供策略使用。实验表明该表征可支撑策略完成记忆密集型任务，且部署时无需 VLM 在环推理。作者指出一个值得注意的发现：workspace token 不仅在计算上更轻量，还带来了优于基线的策略性能。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出一种将 VLM 的语义筛选能力离线蒸馏为轻量潜在记忆的训练范式，通过 workspace token 实现部署期无 VLM 推理的记忆型策略，同时兼顾效率与性能。其意义在于为长时程机器人操作提供了一条区别于 in-the-loop VLM 查询的高效记忆路径。潜在局限包括：对训练阶段 VLM 信息筛选质量的依赖、集合重建损失对历史信息保真度的影响，以及论文摘要未给出的跨任务/跨环境泛化、真实硬件条件下的量化延迟与计算开销等具体指标。

Abstract: Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation, which we call the \textbf{workspace token}, is trained by (1) using a VLM to identify current and historical information necessary for completing a task, then (2) distilling these into the workspace token using a set-reconstruction decoder loss. In both simulation and hardware, we show that the workspace token can be used as a drop-in replacement for observations during deployment, enabling policies to solve memory-intensive tasks without the need for VLM reasoning in-the-loop. Interestingly, we found that workspace tokens are not only more lightweight but also lead to better policy performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种以意图驱动的生成式查询建议框架，通过两阶段优化同时兼顾单个查询的实用性与整个查询列表（slate）的意图覆盖多样性。第一阶段构建意图对齐的SFT数据并设计意图感知多样性奖励；第二阶段进行查询级信用分配，将个体质量信号路由到对应查询token，同时在slate级别共享多样性信号。在大规模生产数据集上的离线评估与在线A/B测试显示CTR、查询质量和意图覆盖均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议的目标是预测用户意图并推荐相关的后续查询，以提升用户参与度。其核心挑战在于：生成的查询列表需要同时满足两个相互冲突的目标——每条查询本身对用户有用（个体质量），而整个列表又需覆盖互不相同的意图（slate级多样性）。现有方法往往难以在二者间取得平衡，容易出现查询冗余、意图覆盖不足或个体质量下降的问题，因此需要一种能显式建模意图并协调个体与列表级目标的优化机制。

Method: 方法采用两阶段优化框架。第一阶段为意图感知多样性建模：构建与意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并引入意图感知多样性奖励（Intent-Aware Diversity Reward）来优化意图覆盖，使模型在生成时考虑列表整体的意图分布。第二阶段为查询级信用分配（query-level credit assignment）：将个体查询的质量信号精确路由到生成该查询的对应token，实现细粒度的信用分配，同时把一个slate级别的多样性信号在整个slate范围内共享，从而将个体质量与列表多样性解耦并进行联合优化。该设计结合了偏好优化/强化学习式的奖励机制与token级信用分配。

Result: 实验在一个大规模生产数据集上进行，结合了离线评估与在线A/B测试。评估关注点击率（CTR）、查询质量以及意图覆盖（intent coverage）等指标。结果表明所提框架在CTR、查询质量和意图覆盖方面相较基线均有提升，验证了双阶段优化在个体质量与多样性之间的平衡效果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出以意图驱动的生成式查询建议框架，通过意图感知多样性奖励与查询级信用分配实现个体质量与slate级多样性的联合优化，并已在大规模生产环境的在线A/B测试中验证有效性。主要贡献在于将意图覆盖显式纳入生成式建议优化，并解决slate内多查询的信用分配粒度问题。潜在局限在于：摘要未披露基线对比细节、离线指标定义及奖励权重的敏感性分析，且意图标注的获取成本与泛化性有待进一步说明。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [220] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"逐层课程学习"(layer-wise curriculum learning)用于高效的大语言模型压缩,通过将模型划分为多个由若干层组成的片段,以课程学习方式(由易到难)实现教师到学生的知识迁移,并结合特征缓存与多线程策略解决跨层特征错位问题。在BERT、GPT-2上实现SOTA压缩性能,GPU显存与训练时间减少超过50%,并在LLaMA家族与Qwen模型上优于其他剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩(如剪枝、蒸馏)的核心挑战在于:完整模型的知识迁移计算开销巨大,且学生模型在优化过程中容易出现误差累积。作者通过理论分析指出存在"累积误差现象"(cumulative error phenomenon),该现象会拖慢收敛并破坏知识迁移的稳定性。因此,需要一种既能加速收敛、又能稳定迁移过程且计算高效的压缩训练框架,以降低显存占用与训练时长。

Method: 核心方法包括三部分:(1)逐层课程学习:将整个模型切分为由多层构成的多个segment,按由易到难的课程顺序进行教师-学生知识迁移,从而在减少一次性计算量的前提下高效完成蒸馏/压缩;(2)理论分析:针对累积误差现象给出分析,论证逐层课程学习能加快收敛并稳定知识迁移;(3)特征缓存与多线程:提出特征缓存机制配合多线程策略,处理跨层特征错位(feature misalignment)问题,最大化GPU利用率。整体上以分段、渐进的方式替代全模型联合优化,兼顾压缩性能与计算效率。

Result: 在多个数据集上进行实验,评估指标涵盖压缩后模型性能、GPU显存占用与训练时长。结果显示:在BERT和GPT-2上,该方法在达到SOTA压缩性能的同时,将GPU显存使用和训练时间均减少50%以上;在相同训练时长下,于LLaMA家族与Qwen模型上优于其他剪枝方法,且GPU显存占用更低。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为:提出面向LLM压缩的逐层课程学习框架,给出累积误差的理论分析以支撑收敛与稳定性优势,并通过特征缓存与多线程策略提升工程效率,在多个模型族与数据集上验证了性能与效率的双重优势。潜在局限在于:论文以LLM压缩(剪枝/蒸馏)为场景,未直接涉及事件相机、事件视觉或脉冲神经网络等方向;此外逐层分段策略对具体分层划分方式、课程难度排序的敏感性,以及在不同规模/架构模型上的泛化能力,摘要中未做充分说明。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [221] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出**块并行（Block Parallelism, BP）**及扩展版本 **CSBP（Context-Sharded Block Parallelism）**，一种针对块扩散语言模型（BDLMs）的新型分布式并行维度。利用BDLM目标函数在目标块上可分解的特性，将每个被噪声污染的块的计算分配到一个独立的rank上，并进一步对共享的干净序列进行上下文分片，从而在长上下文训练中避免重复的干净前缀、保持受污染K/V与梯度本地化，显著提升吞吐并降低显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型（BDLMs）在块间采用自回归依赖、块内采用并行去噪，但长上下文训练受限于两个瓶颈：(1) 分布式注意力（attention）的通信开销；(2) 激活显存占用。传统上下文并行（CP）按位置对"干净+受污染"的合并序列进行分片，需要在各rank间通信共享的干净K/V以及块特定的受污染K/V及其梯度，造成大量冗余通信与显存复制。作者观察到BDLM的训练目标在目标块（target block）之间是可分离的（separates over target blocks），这一性质为按块而非按位置划分计算提供了理论依据，从而引出了更高效的并行方案。

Method: 核心贡献分两层：\n\n1. **块并行（BP）**：新增一个分布式并行维度，将每个"受污染块"（corrupted block）的计算整体分配给单个rank。由于BDLM目标可分解到各目标块，每个rank可独立完成对应块的去噪计算，避免了跨rank的受污染K/V通信。\n\n2. **上下文分片的块并行（CSBP）**：为将BP扩展到超长上下文，进一步把共享的干净序列（clean sequence）沿上下文维度分片到这些rank上。CSBP 保持受污染K/V及其梯度本地（local），避免复制干净前缀（replicated clean prefixes），并完整保留BDLM的训练语义。该设计将通信量与显存开销解耦，同时与块扩散的去噪机制天然契合。

Result: 实验覆盖多类场景与硬件配置：\n\n- **16×H200，256K上下文**：相比最优基线，监督微调（SFT）吞吐提升 **1.18–1.45×**，将自回归模型转换为BDLM提升 **1.27–1.33×**，峰值HBM持平或更低。\n- **512K上下文**：全模型加速达到 **1.61×**。\n- **8×H100**：加速 DFlash2 投机解码器（speculative decoder）训练，512K下达 **2.48×**，1M下达 **7.59×**。\n- **训练质量**：在匹配的12小时 DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT实验中，CSBP 在 **SWE-bench Verified** 与 **Terminal-Bench Lite** 的每个已训练checkpoint上均取得更高通过率，说明加速未损害（甚至提升）下游效果。\n\n代码开源：https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出BP与CSBP两种面向块扩散语言模型的分布式并行策略，利用BDLM目标按目标块可分解的数学性质，将计算按块划分并配合上下文分片，在长上下文训练中同时实现了吞吐提升与显存优化。其意义在于：(1) 为扩散语言模型的长上下文训练提供了新的并行范式，区别于传统CP；(2) 在SFT、AR转BDLM、投机解码器训练等多任务上验证了通用性与可扩展性（至1M上下文）；(3) 保持训练语义与下游质量。潜在局限：效果依赖BDLM目标的可分解结构，可能不直接适用于标准自回归模型；实验集中于特定模型（DiffusionGemma、DFlash2）与高端GPU集群，大规模异构硬件与更广模型族的适用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [222] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对词-文档二部图（bipartite）上的归一化谱共聚类（spectral co-clustering），提出两种基于随机化的 SVD 近似方法以降低计算开销：一是基于随机投影的随机化 SVD（randomized SVD），二是部分 SVD 结合逐元素随机采样（element-wise random sampling）。实验表明两种方法均比全 SVD 基线更快，但效果依赖于矩阵稀疏性：随机投影法更稳健，采样法在较稠密矩阵上更有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类是挖掘词-文档矩阵潜在结构（即同时聚类文档与词汇）的有效工具，但标准归一化谱共聚类依赖奇异值分解（SVD），在高维文本数据上代价高昂。同时，文档簇数与词簇数可能不相等，这种非对称（non-square、簇数不等）情形下的高效近似方法尚缺乏系统研究，因此需要可扩展的随机化近似方案，并明确其在何种数据条件下适用。

Method: 面向二部文本数据的归一化谱共聚类，在文档簇数与词簇数可以不同的设定下给出两种随机近似：(1) 利用随机投影实现随机化 SVD，用低维随机子空间近似主导奇异子空间；(2) 结合部分 SVD（partial SVD）与逐元素随机采样，通过对矩阵元素采样来估计奇异结构。两种方法均用于替代完整 SVD 步骤，从而降低计算复杂度，并在真实与合成数据集上比较其近似质量与运行时间，重点分析其行为随矩阵稀疏性的变化。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上进行评估，以完整 SVD 基线为参照，比较运行时间与近似质量。结果：两种随机化方法相对全 SVD 基线均减少运行时间；但性能依赖于矩阵稀疏性——基于随机投影的方法在所测试设置中更为可靠的近似，而基于采样的方法在较稠密的矩阵上最有用，在本身已稀疏的文本数据上收益有限。结论是随机化谱共聚类的近似方法应根据数据底层结构来选择。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出并比较了两种针对归一化谱共聚类（允许文档簇与词簇数不等）的随机化 SVD 近似方法，量化了运行时间收益与近似稳健性同矩阵稀疏性的关系，指出随机投影法更稳健、采样法适用于较稠密数据。意义在于为大规模词-文档共聚类的可扩展实现提供选择依据。局限在于方法效能高度依赖数据稀疏结构，采样法对稀疏文本收益有限，且未涉及极大规模或流式场景下的表现。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [223] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），将卷积映射为频域乘法，复用无线设备中已有的频率混频器以单次通带完成多通道卷积，从而在极低功耗下实现深度CNN推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能手机、可穿戴设备、无人机等边缘端点直接运行AI模型可获得低延迟、可扩展性与数据隐私，但这些设备难以承载现代神经网络的计算需求。现有方案依赖额外边缘加速器，而它们会进一步增加本已受限的体积、重量、功耗与成本（SWaP-C）负担。论文的动机是寻找一种无需新增计算硬件的替代路径：利用每台无线设备本就具备的频率混频器在时域上相乘、天然等价于频域卷积的特性，把通信硬件重塑为推理硬件。

Method: 核心思想是将CNN的多通道卷积映射为频率音（frequency tones），由无源混频器在一次通带内并行执行——时域乘法对应频域卷积。权重通过无线链路在空中传输到边缘设备，模拟硬件与通信模块共享，因此边缘端只需承担数据准备与读出开销，而不必集成专用数字处理器。系统命名为RF-CNN，属于模拟/射频域计算与通信-计算一体化的架构创新。

Result: 论文进行了实验验证：RF-CNN可运行最深九层、参数量高达2640万的深度CNN，任务涵盖无线信号分类、图像分类以及可控图像生成，性能接近全精度基准。能效方面，每次乘累加（MAC）仅约0.72飞焦，比在额外数字处理器上执行低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种复用现有无线通信硬件进行CNN推理的新范式，兼顾深度模型能力与极低能耗，暗示已部署的无线基础设施可将高效、前沿的AI推理能力带给其已连接的数十亿设备。潜在局限包括：模拟/射频计算在精度、噪声与动态范围上的约束；权重的空中传输带来的可靠性、时延与安全性问题；以及任务通用性与面向不同无线频段/制式的可移植性等仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [224] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非平衡动力学平均场理论（DMFT），用于描述循环神经网络（RNN）在学习过程中从混沌动力学向稳定任务相关动力学的转变，并预测该转变的分岔行为与训练过程中网络输出的时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN 训练会从根本上重塑其内在动力学，使初始混沌活动转变为稳定的、与任务相关的行为。然而，对于这一转变过程——尤其是"学习如何改变网络动力学"的机制——缺乏解析性的理论刻画。理解学习过程中动力学转变的发生时点与临界条件，对于解释 RNN 的可训练性、稳定性以及泛化行为具有重要意义。

Method: 作者构建了一个非平衡动力学平均场理论（non-equilibrium DMFT）来追踪学习过程中的动力学演化。核心思路是：缓慢的、由反馈驱动的学习过程会产生一个随训练演化的"有效反馈强度"（effective feedback strength），该量驱动网络从一个混沌解经过分岔（DMFT 解的分岔）进入稳定区。通过推导学习全程的两时间关联函数（two-time correlation function），作者识别出临界反馈强度，以及依赖学习率的临界时间（critical time），用于区分混沌与稳定两个阶段。理论指出，该转变源于不断增长的学习到的反馈结构对有效动力学景观的渐进式形变（deformation of an effective dynamical landscape）。

Result: 该理论从初始未训练状态出发，能够预测训练过程中网络输出的时间演化，并在数值上与直接数值模拟结果取得定量一致。文中给出了临界反馈强度与学习率相关的临界时间这两个可解析刻画的关键量，用以标定混沌—稳定转变。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于为 RNN 学习中的混沌到稳定动力学转变提供了非平衡 DMFT 解析框架，揭示了反馈强度与学习率共同决定转变时点的机制，并成功预测了训练过程中网络输出的时间演化。意义在于将学习动力学与统计物理中的相变/分岔理论联系起来。潜在局限在于该理论基于平均场近似（通常假设大规模网络、特定连接结构或统计均匀性），其适用性可能受限于网络规模、学习规则类型及任务结构，且摘要中未说明实验所用具体任务与数据集。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [225] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦分层高斯过程（pFedHGP），通过将每个客户端的隐函数分解为共享全局成分、共享核结构的客户端特化偏差以及灵活局部残差，实现异构客户端间的联邦概率回归与分类，在保持数据本地化的同时提供完整预测分布以支持不确定性感知决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习场景下，客户端数据分布异构（non-IID），传统联邦方法多面向点估计且难以刻画不确定性。同时，集中式高斯过程需要汇聚原始数据，存在隐私与通信瓶颈。该文旨在解决如何在数据不离开本地的前提下，对异构客户端进行具有不确定性量化的概率建模（回归与分类），并兼顾全局知识的共享与客户端个性化的表达。

Method: 核心是分层函数分解：每个客户端的隐函数 = （i）共享全局成分 + （ii）共享全局核结构的客户端特化偏差 + （iii）灵活局部残差，从而在保留共性的同时允许个性化。采用稀疏诱导变量（inducing variables）近似降低计算复杂度，并使用联邦变分推断（federated variational inference）进行训练：原始数据保留在本地，服务器仅同步共享成分的低维充分统计量。输出为完整预测分布，天然支持不确定性感知决策。此外给出 Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model（ILMM）视角，将分层结构联系到多输出高斯过程，以建模相关传感器。

Result: 在应用研究中，pFedHGP 于压机吨位监测（press tonnage monitoring）故障分类任务上取得完美分类，且仅使用 13.77% 的带标签周期；在联邦空气质量建模中，无需集中各站点级时间序列即可恢复地理分区（geographic zones）。论文以概率回归与分类任务为主，通过不确定性感知的预测分布及上述应用案例验证方法有效性。
