Abstract: Financial sentiment measures are widely used in empirical finance, but it remains unclear whether general-purpose large language models (LLMs) improve on existing finance-specific methods. This paper evaluates twelve sentiment models, including dictionary-based methods, finance-specific transformers, and open-source LLMs, using two criteria: linguistic validity and economic validity. We find that general-purpose LLMs achieve classification performance comparable to finance-specific transformer models without task-specific fine-tuning. However, higher classification accuracy does not translate into stronger economic relationships. Several models produce sentiment measures that are significantly associated with earnings surprises, but none is significantly associated with next-day stock returns. Model performance is strongest for announcements with large earnings beats or misses and substantially weaker for announcements with more moderate earnings surprises. These findings suggest that financial sentiment captures information about firms' economic performance but has limited ability to explain short-run market reactions

</details>


### [283] [Towards a Unified Modality-Agnostic Multimodal Framework for Cognitive Workload Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20199)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Calvin Joseph,Giorgos Giannakakis,Raul Fernandez Rojas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的、模态无关的分层 Transformer 架构，用于在单一模型中处理多模态异构生物信号，以测量认知工作负荷，并系统性地评估了 5 种生理信号的全部 31 种组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知工作负荷反映任务执行中的心理努力程度，是设计自适应人机系统的核心。虽然利用生物信号测量认知负荷已有大量研究，但针对异构生物信号模态融合（multimodal fusion）的实证研究仍然不足，尤其缺乏对"模态组合究竟能否带来增益"以及"如何用统一模型高效处理异构信号"的系统性探索。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为多模态生物信号融合提供架构与实证依据。

Method: 提出一种统一、模态无关（modality-agnostic）的分层 Transformer 架构，能够在同一个模型内处理异构生物信号模态，避免为每种模态单独设计网络或采用后期融合（late fusion）。涉及的 5 种模态为：心电图（ECG）、皮肤电活动（EDA）、呼吸（RESP）、外周血氧饱和度（SpO₂）和脑电（EEG）。模型通过分层 Transformer 结构对多模态时序信号进行统一编码与融合，实现单一模型下的多模态处理，而非各模态独立分支后拼接。

Result: 采用留一被试交叉验证（leave-one-subject-out, LOSO），覆盖三种认知任务：抽象推理（IQ）、算术解题（MATH）和游戏任务（GAME），并评估了 5 种模态的全部 31 种组合。实验结果提示：(i) EEG 是最强的单一模态，在 IQ、GAME 及混合全部任务的 ALL 设置中均排名最高；(ii) 增加模态数量并不能持续提升性能；(iii) 五模态全组合在 IQ 上取得最高 Average 分数 73.02%，在四个评估设置（IQ、MATH、GAME、ALL）平均后为 68.08%；(iv) 相比后期融合方案，所提方法模型规模减少约 50%，推理时间更低。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于：提出一个统一、模态无关的分层 Transformer 架构以单模型处理异构生物信号，并首次（作为试点研究）系统性地穷举评估 5 种模态的 31 种组合。结果表明 EEG 为主要贡献模态，多模态融合并非总是带来增益，而所提架构在保持竞争力的同时显著降低模型规模与推理时间，体现出较高的计算效率。局限在于这是一项试点研究（pilot study），被试规模与任务范围有限，结论的泛化性有待更大规模验证；"更多模态不持续提升性能"这一现象也提示需要更深入的模态选择与融合机制研究。

Abstract: Cognitive workload reflects the mental effort required during task performance and is central to the design of adaptive human-machine systems. The use of biosignals to measure cognitive workload has been extensively researched and documented; however, studies examining the effects of combining heterogeneous biosignal modalities for this purpose remain limited. To provide insight into this area, we developed a unified, modality-agnostic, hierarchical Transformer-based architecture to process heterogeneous biosignal modalities within a single model. We use this framework in a pilot study evaluating all $31$ possible combinations of five modalities: Electrocardiogram (ECG), Electrodermal Activity (EDA), Respiration (RESP), Peripheral Oxygen Saturation (SpO$_2$), and Electroencephalogram (EEG), under leave-one-subject-out validation across three cognitively distinct tasks: abstract reasoning (IQ), arithmetic problem solving (MATH), and a game task (GAME). In this pilot setting, the results suggest that: (i) EEG is the strongest single modality, ranking highest in IQ, GAME, and the pooled ALL setting, where samples from all three tasks are combined; (ii) adding more modalities does not consistently improve performance; (iii) the full five-modality combination achieves the highest \textit{Average} score of $73.02%$ on IQ and $68.08%$ when the \textit{Average} scores are averaged over the four evaluation settings: IQ, MATH, GAME, and ALL; and (iv) the proposed method reduces model size by approximately $50%$ compared with late-fusion alternatives while maintaining a lower inference time.

</details>


### [284] [Scene-Conditioned Relation Routing for urban cellular activity forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20209)
*Qingzhong Li,Jingye Lin,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Xinjun Pei,Ming Yan,Fei Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCRR-Net 提出一种"场景条件化的空间关系路由"框架，利用城市上下文信息同时控制空间依赖选择与跨任务知识迁移，统一建模 SMS、移动网络流量与通话活动等多信号时空预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市蜂窝活动预测需要联合建模异构时空信号（短信、网络流量、通话活动）。现有方法将时间建模、空间关系学习与多信号预测相互割裂，且依赖固定的图结构或静态的多任务学习方案，难以适应不断变化的城市场景，缺乏对动态空间依赖与任务间关系的自适应能力。

Method: SCRR-Net 由四个模块组成：上下文编码器（context encoder）提取城市场景上下文；空间图专家路由模块（spatial graph expert routing）依据上下文动态选择空间依赖（即选用不同的图专家）；时间 Transformer 编码器建模时序依赖；任务知识路由模块（task knowledge routing）在场景条件下进行跨任务知识迁移。核心创新是以上下文为条件，联合调控空间关系路由与任务间知识流动，取代固定的图结构与静态多任务学习。

Result: 在 Milano 与 Trento 两个城市蜂窝数据集上，针对 SMS、网络流量与通话活动三项预测任务进行了实验。结果表明 SCRR-Net 在各项任务上均持续优于对比方法，并且其路由行为具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出场景条件化的空间关系与任务知识双重路由框架，将空间依赖选择与跨任务迁移统一于城市上下文控制之下，提升了多信号城市活动预测的适应性与可解释性。潜在局限：摘要未披露具体指标、基线方法及路由开销等细节，且方法面向城市蜂窝时空预测，与事件相机/事件视觉、目标检测等方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Urban cellular activity forecasting requires jointly modeling heterogeneous spatiotemporal signals, including SMS usage, mobile network traffic, and call activity. Existing methods often separate temporal modeling, spatial relation learning, and multi-signal prediction, relying on fixed graph structures or static multi-task learning schemes, which limits their adaptability to changing urban scenes. We propose SCRR-Net, a scene-conditioned spatial relation routing framework in which urban contextual information jointly controls spatial dependency selection and cross-task knowledge transfer. SCRR-Net includes a context encoder, a spatial graph expert routing module, a temporal Transformer encoder, and a task knowledge routing module. Experiments on the Milano and Trento datasets demonstrate that SCRR-Net consistently outperforms competing methods on SMS, network traffic, and call activity forecasting, while providing interpretable routing behaviors.

</details>


### [285] [Subdomain-aware representation compression for pretrained image embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20213)
*Poowanut Niamluang,Jittat Fakcharoenphol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将降维技术（PCA与LDA）从传统的"全数据集统一降维"扩展到"子域级表示压缩"，以图像域为对象验证其在边缘设备ML中的空间与计算效率，并发现降维后不仅提升效率，还意外地带来精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维是提升空间效率的经典手段，但通常对整个数据集统一施加。现实数据往往由多个子域（subdomain）构成，不同子域的特征分布差异较大，统一降维难以针对性地保留各子域的判别信息。对于资源受限的边缘设备机器学习应用，如何在压缩表示的同时保持甚至提升判别能力，是一个关键问题。作者由此提出：能否在子域粒度上进行表示压缩，并考察其对效率与精度的双重影响。

Method: 作者在图像域上采用两类经典线性降维方法——主成分分析（PCA）与线性判别分析（LDA）——进行子域表示压缩。核心做法是并非对全数据集施加同一投影，而是针对各个子域（subdomain）分别执行降维，从而获得压缩后的子域表示（subdomain representation）。所使用的表示基于嵌入（embedding），降维直接在嵌入空间上进行；在此基础上进一步评估了压缩表示的特征质量。此外，通过实验考察了压缩表示的迁移学习（transfer learning）能力。

Result: 实验在图像域数据集上进行，评估维度包括：空间与计算复杂度、分类精度，以及迁移学习表现。结果表明：（1）子域降维确实带来空间和计算复杂度上的改善，符合边缘设备ML的预期；（2）相比直接使用完整嵌入（full-embedding）的做法，降维后的表示反而取得精度提升；（3）压缩表示在迁移学习任务中展现出可用的迁移能力。作者给出的解释是，降维过程实质上起到了子域特征提取的作用，因而有助于提升判别性能。

Conclusion: 该工作将降维的应用范围从整体数据集推广到子域表示压缩，验证了PCA/LDA在边缘设备场景中兼顾效率与精度的可行性，并提出"降维即子域特征提取"的解释视角，同时展示了压缩表示的迁移学习潜力。局限在于：目前仅在图像域的嵌入表示上验证，使用PCA/LDA等线性方法；摘要未给出具体数据集、精度数值与迁移学习的详细设置，"降维提升精度"的现象也缺乏更深入的理论或消融分析。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction is a well-known technique for improving space efficiency, typically applied uniformly across an entire dataset. This paper investigates the possibilities of using dimensionality reduction techniques for subdomain representation compression. We explore standard techniques such as Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear discriminant analysis (LDA) in image domains. The results not only demonstrate the expected improvements in space and computation complexity crucial for edge-device ML applications but also show improvements in accuracy over direct full-embedding procedure. One possible explanation is that dimensionality reduction effectively extracts subdomain features. We also performed experiments to demonstrate transfer learning capabilities using the compressed representations.

</details>


### [286] [Explaining spatial information flow in short-term traffic forecasting models using a gated graph attention network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20217)
*Yue Li,Shujuan Chen,Ying Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在 ST-MetaNet 的图注意力层（GAT）中引入可学习的门控（gate），在每个传感器、每个时间步上自适应决定从邻居节点获取多少状态信息。通过对门控施加正则化实现渐进式消融，从而同时获得可解释的空间信息流分析和架构组件的必要性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期交通流预测依赖 GAT 来建模空间依赖，但 GAT 层常被宣称"捕获邻居影响"却鲜有验证，因为注意力权重没有可比的实测物理量作为参照。这导致两个开放问题：模型应如何解释，以及哪些组件是真正必要的。交通预测关乎路网实时监控与控制的可靠性，因此需要一种既能解释空间信息流、又能对组件必要性做分级评估的机制。

Method: 作者在 GAT 层中加入一个门，逐传感器、逐时间步学习传感器更新状态中来自邻居而非自身的比例。门控经正则化后可逐步撤除邻居信息，形成分级的（graded）消融形式：轻度正则化近似完整模型，强度越高则邻居信息被撤除越多。方法应用于 ST-MetaNet——其编码器和解码器各含一个 GAT 层，夹在两个循环层之间。模型在英国战略路网 498 个环形线圈检测器上、一整年记录上训练。

Result: 在 498 个环形检测器、一年数据上的实验显示：门控分配更多邻居信息给交通负荷更重的传感器，并遵循日周期与周周期规律，与"相邻位置在繁忙时耦合更强"一致。轻度正则化略微提升精度，正则化更强时精度下降（因撤除了模型所需信息）。编码器门先于解码器门关闭，但直接消融对该顺序提出质疑：单独移除任一 GAT 层，精度至少与保留两层相当；同时移除两层才显著下降，说明两层大体冗余而非各自不可或缺。

Conclusion: 主要贡献：提出门控 GAT 作为可解释且可分级消融的空间依赖机制；带来小幅精度提升、空间信息流在何时何地流动的解释，以及架构所需层的证据。意义在于将常见的"注意力即邻居影响"假设落实为可验证的量。局限：其"顺序先关"发现与直接消融结论存在张力，说明门控行为不完全等价于组件必要性；实验仅限单一数据集/路网与 ST-MetaNet 架构，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Short-term traffic forecasting supports real-time monitoring and control of road networks, and graph attention networks (GAT) are the standard means of representing spatial dependence in these models. GAT layers are widely described as capturing the influence of neighbouring locations, but this is seldom verified, because the attention weights offered in support cannot be compared against any measured quantity. That leaves two questions open, how the model should be explained and which of its components are necessary. We address this by adding a gate to the GAT layer which learns, at every sensor and every time step, what share of a sensor's updated state is drawn from its neighbours rather than from itself. Regularising the gate withdraws neighbour information progressively and thereby provides a graded form of ablation. We apply the gated GAT to ST-MetaNet, whose encoder and decoder each place one GAT layer between two recurrent layers, and train it on one calendar year of records from 498 loop detectors on the strategic road network of England. The gate assigns a larger share of neighbour information to sensors carrying heavier traffic and follows the daily and weekly cycle of travel, consistent with adjacent locations being more strongly coupled when busy. Mild regularisation improves accuracy slightly, and accuracy declines at higher strengths as the penalty withdraws information the model needs. The encoder gate closes before the decoder gate, but direct ablation qualifies that ordering. Removing either GAT layer alone leaves accuracy at least as good as keeping both, whereas removing both degrades it substantially, so the two layers are largely redundant rather than either being indispensable. The gated GAT therefore yields a modest accuracy gain, an explanation of where and when spatial information flows, and evidence on which layers the architecture requires.

</details>


### [287] [Is It Still Worth Training a Classical Model in the Era of LLMs? A Crossover Benchmark on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20218)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"标注数据交叉点N*"这一量化指标，用来回答一个实际问题：面对表格型业务预测任务，应该直接提示冻结的LLM（无需训练），还是收集标注数据训练经典模型？结果发现训练获胜极快——交叉点中位数仅约为训练集的6%，即使给LLM最优提示配置，经典模型在86%的情况下用现有数据量即可胜过小型冻结LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如今微软Excel Copilot、Claude for Excel等主流表格工具已内置LLM零样本打标能力，这让许多标注昂贵的业务预测场景面临抉择：是提示冻结的LLM，还是收集数据训练模型、若训练又需要多少数据？论文的动机正是为这一现实决策提供定量依据，而非依赖直觉或个例经验。该问题重要在于：标注成本高、业务表格普遍存在，选错路径会显著浪费资源或牺牲精度。论文引入N*（训练误差曲线首次超越冻结LLM平坦误差时的训练集规模）把定性的"要不要训练"转化为可测量的量。

Method: 论文的核心是可量化的交叉点分析框架。实验聚合了两个来源：(1) 126份独立的学生评测，覆盖小型GPT模型在8种提示配置下、18个表格数据集上的表现；(2) 六个经典模型族（含梯度提升等）的权威幂律学习曲线。通过将LLM"无训练、因此误差曲线平坦"的特性与经典模型随数据量呈幂律下降的学习曲线相交，计算N*。方法中还包含若干稳健性设计：以"oracle最优提示配置"作为LLM的乐观上界（对LLM有利的假设）；评估"最少标注子集"下的胜负；对few-shot上下文示例与真实训练进行对比（考察误差-样本数是否服从幂律）；由独立实施者重跑同一协议以评估方法方差；并设计受控探针检验LLM是否依赖可识别的特征名语义。

Result: 在18个表格数据集、8种提示配置下：即使给LLM其最佳的oracle提示配置，训练后的经典模型仍在86%的情况下仅用现有数据量即超过小型冻结LLM；在40%的情况下，经典模型用所评估的最少标注子集即可获胜。观测到的交叉点中位数约为训练集的6%。关于few-shot：上下文少样本示例的表现不像训练——误差与样本数之间不服从幂律。协议可复现性方面，不同独立实施者重跑结果变异系数为0.148。受控探针表明LLM依赖可识别的特征名语义，这暗示交叉点估计对LLM偏保守（作者明确不主张记忆化机制）。总体结论：对于典型业务表格，收集几百个标签并训练梯度提升模型是证据支持的选择。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是用N*这一交叉点指标，将"提示冻结LLM vs. 收集数据训练"的选择从经验直觉变为可量化、可复现的实证问题，并在广泛的表格数据集与提示配置上给出训练快速胜出的证据，同时给出可操作的实务建议（收集几百标签训练GBM）。意义在于为表格工具的LLM零样本打标能力提供了成本-精度权衡的定量基准，并揭示few-shot上下文示例不等价于训练（不服从幂律）。局限包括：作者明确不主张LLM依赖记忆化，交叉点估计偏保守；结论主要限于小型GPT模型与18个表格数据集；协议存在一定方差（CV 0.148）；且该研究聚焦表格预测，与事件相机、小目标检测、SNN等视觉任务无直接关联。

Abstract: Large language models can label a tabular row from a plain-English description with no training - a capability now shipping in mainstream spreadsheet tools such as Microsoft Copilot in Excel and Anthropic's Claude for Excel - raising a practical question for the many business prediction problems where labels are expensive: should you prompt a frozen LLM, or collect data and train a model - and if so, how much data? We quantify the answer with the labeled-data crossover N*, the training-set size at which a trained classical model's learning curve overtakes a frozen LLM's training-free (and therefore flat) error. Aggregating 126 independent student evaluations of small GPT models under eight prompting configurations across 18 tabular datasets, paired with authoritative power-law learning curves for six classical model families, we find that training wins fast: even given an oracle choice of its best prompt configuration, a trained classical model beats the small frozen LLM using no more labeled data than is already on hand in 86% of cases, and wins by the smallest labeled subset we evaluate in 40%, with the observed crossover at a median of ~6% of the training set. In-context few-shot examples do not behave like training - error versus shot count does not follow a power law - and the same protocol re-run by independent implementers varies with a coefficient of variation of 0.148. A controlled probe indicates the LLM depends on recognizable feature-name semantics, which plausibly makes our crossover a conservative estimate (we do not claim memorization). For a typical business table, the evidence is clear: collect a few hundred labels and train a gradient-boosted model.

</details>


### [288] [Accuracy Is Not Enough: A Cross-Architecture Audit of Demographic Bias in Deep Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20249)
*Dang Quang Minh,Nguyen Dung Son,Nguyen Huu Loi,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次对现代深度知识追踪（DKT）模型进行跨架构的人口统计学公平性审计，比较 DKT、DKVMN、SAKT、AKT 四种架构在标准、重加权、对抗去偏三种训练范式和两个数据集上的偏差表现，揭示"最准的模型往往最偏"以及标准去偏手段不可靠的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪模型隐式判定学生对技能的掌握程度，从而驱动自适应学习系统的决策，若存在人口统计学偏差将直接影响教育公平。然而现有公平性证据几乎全部来自贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT），支撑现代系统的深度模型缺乏可比的跨架构公平性审计。该工作旨在填补这一空白，并回应近期关于公平性指标不稳定的批评。

Method: 采用四类深度知识追踪架构（DKT、DKVMN、SAKT、AKT）作为审计对象，在三种训练范式下训练：标准训练、样本重加权（reweighting）、对抗去偏（adversarial debiasing，包含完整对抗强度下的随机水平探测与弱强度阳性对照以排除"无效探针"）。评估使用 ABROCA（AUC 曲线下面积差）作为核心公平性指标，并结合学生级 bootstrap 置信区间与置换检验以缓解公平性指标的稳定性问题。通过仅消融 AKT 的 item 级 Rasch 嵌入进行归因分析。

Result: 实验使用两个含人口统计学元数据的公开数据集：Eedi（约 1590 万次交互）与 OULAD（预处理后约 16.7 万次交互）。主要发现：(i) 偏差真实存在但依赖语境——所有架构在 Eedi 上均表现出显著的社会经济 ABROCA（0.018–0.023，p<0.005），经济弱势群体的分组 AUC 更低；而性别偏差在 OULAD 上对四分之三架构经多重比较校正后仍显著，但在 Eedi 上可忽略。(ii) 最准确的架构偏差最大——AKT 借助 item 级 Rasch 嵌入获得约 4 个 AUC 点提升，同时呈现最大的社会经济 ABROCA，在配对 bootstrap 下显著高于其他所有架构（p≤0.002）；仅消融 Rasch 嵌入即同时消除精度增益与超额偏差。(iii) 标准去偏手段不可靠——在保持精度的所有配置中，重加权与对抗去偏几乎不改变 ABROCA，尽管对抗器在完整反转强度下已处于随机水平，且弱强度阳性对照排除了探针失效的可能。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是首次对深度知识追踪模型进行系统的跨架构、跨训练范式的公平性审计，提出了结合 ABROCA、学生级 bootstrap 与置换检验的稳健评估流程，并给出"精度—偏差耦合"（Rasch 嵌入同时驱动精度与偏差）与"去偏失效"两大关键结论，对教育 AI 的公平性实践具有警示意义。局限包括：仅覆盖两个数据集与四类架构，主要聚焦社会经济与性别的两元敏感属性，且未提出新的有效去偏方法；对抗去偏失效的机制（如对抗器与预测器优化张力）仍待进一步剖析。

Abstract: Deep knowledge tracing (DKT) models implicitly decide which students an adaptive system believes have mastered a skill, yet almost all evidence on their demographic fairness comes from Bayesian knowledge tracing; the deep models that power modern systems have received no comparable cross-architecture audit. We close this gap: four architectures (DKT, DKVMN, SAKT, AKT) trained under three regimes (standard, reweighting, adversarial) on two public datasets with demographic metadata, Eedi (15.9M interactions) and OULAD (167k after preprocessing), evaluated with ABROCA, student-level bootstrap confidence intervals, and permutation tests addressing recent critiques of fairness-metric instability. Three findings emerge. (i) Bias is real but context-dependent: every architecture shows a significant socioeconomic ABROCA on Eedi (0.018-0.023, $p<0.005$), with per-group AUC lower for economically disadvantaged students, while gender bias is significant on OULAD for three of four architectures after multiplicity correction yet negligible on Eedi. (ii) The most accurate architecture is the most biased: AKT gains about 4 AUC points from item-level Rasch embeddings and shows the largest socioeconomic ABROCA, exceeding every other architecture under a paired bootstrap ($p\leq0.002$); ablating only the Rasch embeddings removes the accuracy gain and the excess bias together. (iii) Standard mitigation is unreliable: reweighting and adversarial debiasing leave ABROCA essentially unchanged in every configuration that preserves accuracy, even though the adversary is pinned at chance at full reversal strength and a weak-strength positive control rules out a dead probe.

</details>


### [289] [How Far Can Sub-3B Open Language Models Go in Zero-Shot Essay Scoring on an 8 GB Consumer GPU?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20250)
*Nguyen Dung Son,Dang Quang Minh,Nguyen Huu Loi,Truong Viet Vu,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在完全本地、8GB消费级GPU、FP16精度下运行sub-3B开源指令微调模型（Qwen2.5 0.5B/1.5B/3B、SmolLM2 1.7B）进行零样本作文自动评分的可行性，提出"评分标准分解提示（rubric-decomposed prompting）+ Multi-Trait Specialization的min-max归一化聚合"这一配置，在ASAP-AES全部8个prompt上取得macro QWK 0.388，并诚实指出其远低于人工评分者间一致性上限（0.769）和纯长度基线（0.523），定位为形成性、有人类监督的反馈工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本作文自动评分（AES）研究多以专有API大模型为对象，但在最需要自动评分的场景（如公立学校在严格隐私法规下批改数千篇作文）中，将学生作文上传至第三方API往往不可接受。因此核心问题是：当模型必须是本地运行、参数量小于3B、FP16精度、单张8GB消费级GPU时，还剩余多少评分能力？该工作填补了"隐私约束+算力约束"下AES能力边界的空白。

Method: 在受控实验设计下比较两个模型家族四个指令微调模型：Qwen2.5（0.5B/1.5B/3B）与SmolLM2（1.7B），在ASAP-AES的全部8个prompt上评估。关键设计包括：对比"评分标准分解提示（rubric-decomposed prompting）"与"整体式提示（holistic prompting）"；提出Multi-Trait Specialization，将各trait分数映射到prompt评分区间，并采用min-max归一化聚合（batch min-max 与 mean 两种聚合方式）；针对评分者校准脆弱性问题，通过冻结归一化统计量实现部署现实性。统计上使用bootstrap置信区间与Holm校正的配对检验。

Result: 在单张8GB消费级GPU、FP16下完成全部实验。三项主要发现：(i) 在batch min-max聚合下，rubric-decomposed prompting对每个模型均优于holistic prompting（但Qwen2.5-3B在某一个prompt上显著下降）；在mean聚合下，两个无关模型家族在1.5–1.7B规模上差距在0.01以内。(ii) 将trait分数映射到prompt评分区间对评分者校准高度脆弱：某模型把trait压缩到0–10中的2–4窄带，朴素mean聚合失效，而Multi-Trait Specialization的min-max归一化将其修复（macro QWK从0.204提升到0.388），且在30篇留出作文上冻结统计量后波动在0.03以内。(iii) 在12个配置中有11个出现带符号误差随作文长度下降的趋势，这与大模型评判者所报告的长度/冗长偏置相反；归一化rubric分解对校准良好的模型基本拉平了该斜率。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于：系统刻画了sub-3B本地开源模型在隐私敏感、算力受限场景下的零样本AES能力边界，提出并验证了rubric分解与Multi-Trait Specialization归一化聚合的有效性，并揭示了与长度偏置相反的误差-长度趋势这一新现象。作者诚实定位：最佳本地配置macro QWK仅0.388，既远低于人工评分者间上限0.769，也低于纯长度基线0.523，因此明确主张sub-3B本地模型应严格用于形成性、有人类监督的反馈，而非高风险终结性评分。局限包括模型规模与家族范围有限（4个模型、2个家族）、仅评估ASAP-AES单一语料、QWK绝对性能较低，以及需依赖留出集冻结归一化统计量。

Abstract: Zero-shot essay scoring with large language models is usually demonstrated with proprietary API models, yet the settings where automated scoring is most needed, such as public schools grading thousands of essays under strict privacy rules, are often those where sending student writing to a third-party API is unacceptable. We ask how much capability survives when the model must be a sub-3B open model running fully locally in FP16, with a controlled study of four instruction-tuned models from two families (Qwen2.5 at 0.5B/1.5B/3B, SmolLM2 at 1.7B) on all eight ASAP-AES prompts on a single 8 GB consumer GPU, with bootstrap confidence intervals, Holm-corrected paired tests, and deployment-realistic variants of the key design choices. Three findings emerge. (i) Rubric-decomposed prompting beats holistic prompting for every model under batch min-max aggregation (though Qwen2.5-3B drops significantly on one prompt), and under mean aggregation two unrelated families land within 0.01 at the 1.5-1.7B scale. (ii) Mapping trait scores into the prompt range is fragile to grader calibration: one model compresses traits into a narrow low band (2-4 on 0-10) and naive mean aggregation collapses, while the min-max normalization of Multi-Trait Specialization repairs it (macro QWK 0.204 to 0.388) and stays within 0.03 when its statistics are frozen on 30 held-out essays. (iii) Signed error falls with essay length in eleven of twelve configurations, opposite to the verbosity bias reported for large LLM judges; normalized rubric decomposition largely flattens this slope for well-calibrated models. We anchor results honestly: the best local configuration (0.388) remains far below both the human inter-rater ceiling (0.769) and a length-only baseline (0.523), so we position sub-3B local models strictly for formative, human-supervised feedback.

</details>


### [290] [Improving Online Reinforcement Learning via Bidirectional Behavior Prior Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20268)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Jiaji Shen,Ning Jia,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双向行为先验蒸馏（B2PD）算法，用动作价值先验引导条件变分自编码器（CVAE）生成高价值行为支持集，并将专家行为先验蒸馏回策略，从而在在线强化学习中提升样本效率并稳定策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习算法普遍存在样本效率低、学习动态不稳定的问题，其根源在于critic估计误差被贪婪策略更新不断放大。现有基于行为先验的方法依赖离线预训练，从固定数据集中学习行为模型并用策略先验约束在线更新，但离线数据集质量有限，难以提供能有效指导策略更新的高价值策略；缺乏专家轨迹会显著损害在线策略学习，导致样本效率低、性能次优。因此需要一种不依赖高质量离线专家数据、能在在线学习过程中提供高质量行为支持的机制。

Method: B2PD 摒弃传统行为先验思路，核心是"双向知识流"框架：(1) 利用动作价值先验（action-value prior）引导条件变分自编码器（CVAE）生成高价值行为支持集（high-value behavior support set），而非直接从固定离线数据集中采样行为；(2) 将由此得到的专家行为先验蒸馏进智能体，减少低效探索；(3) 建立双向知识流机制，使价值信息与行为先验相互引导，在在线学习过程中持续提供高质量行为支持，从而抑制critic误差的放大，实现稳定、有原则、样本高效的策略优化。

Result: 论文在状态（state-based）和像素（pixel-based）两类任务上进行了实证评估。结果表明，B2PD 在保持策略优化稳定性的同时显著提升了样本效率。更广泛地，实验验证了在在线学习中强制引入高质量行为支持可以有效缓解critic引起的误差放大。（注：摘要未给出具体数据集名称、对比基线及数值指标，详细结果需查阅正文。）

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出 B2PD，用价值引导的CVAE生成高价值行为支持并蒸馏为专家行为先验，构建双向知识流机制，摆脱对高质量离线专家轨迹的依赖，提升在线RL的样本效率与训练稳定性。其意义在于揭示了"高质量行为支持可缓解critic误差放大"这一机制，使结构化行为先验以原则化的方式指导策略更新。局限方面，摘要未明确报告方法对CVAE训练稳定性、动作价值先验估计精度的依赖程度，也未给出计算开销、超参数敏感性及与先进基线在大规模基准上的定量对比。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms frequently exhibit poor sample efficiency and unstable learning dynamics, stemming from systematic critic estimation errors that are exacerbated by greedy policy updates. Existing behavior-prior reinforcement learning methods attempt to alleviate this issue by relying on offline pre-training to learn behavior models from fixed datasets and using policy priors to constrain online policy updates. However, the limited quality of offline datasets often hinders the ability to provide high-value policies that can effectively guide policy updates. The absence of expert trajectories significantly impairs online policy learning, leading to low sample efficiency and suboptimal performance. To address these challenges, we depart from conventional behavior prior approaches and propose a Bidirectional Behavior Prior Distillation (B2PD) algorithm. B2PD leverages action-value priors to guide a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) in generating a high-value behavior support set. The resulting expert behavior priors are further distilled into the agent, effectively reducing inefficient exploration and enabling stable policy optimization, while establishing a bidirectional knowledge flow mechanism. Empirical evaluations on both state- and pixel-based tasks verify that B2PD substantially improves sample efficiency while maintaining stable policy optimization. More broadly, this work shows that enforcing high-quality behavioral support during online learning effectively mitigates critic-induced error amplification, enabling structured behavior priors to guide policy updates in a principled and sample-efficient manner.

</details>


### [291] [Placement Is Free, Composition Is Not: The Latin Square as a Provably-Balanced Construction for Heterogeneous Sequence-Mixer Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20269)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Aether-7B-5Attn（6.59B 参数、约 2.98B 激活的 MoE 模型），其 49 层包含 7 种序列混合机制，按 7×7 拉丁方排列，使每种机制在每一行与每一列中各出现一次，从而消除"机制位置"这一混杂因素。作者构建参数匹配的 4×4 拉丁方代理模型（16 层、700.9M 参数、8 个随机种子）进行受控研究，结论是：性能主要取决于"异构机制在深度上分布"，而非具体的排列顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 GPT 以来，绝大多数 Transformer 在每一层重复相同的注意力机制，这一设计更多是约定俗成而非经过验证的结论。当把多种序列混合器（sequence mixer）组合进同一堆栈时，性能提升可能来自机制类型的选择，也可能来自其放置位置，二者纠缠导致因果归因困难。论文旨在解耦"机制选择"与"位置安排"对模型性能的贡献，回答"异构堆栈的性能提升到底是不是排列方式带来的"这一基础问题。

Method: 核心设计是"拉丁方"序列混合器排布：在 Aether-7B-5Attn 中，49 层的 7 种序列混合机制构成 7×7 拉丁方，每种机制在每行（深度分组）和每列中各出现恰好一次，保证深度上的均衡暴露并消除位置混杂。为进行受控评估，作者构建参数匹配的代理模型：4 种机制构成 16 层的 4×4 拉丁方，参数量对齐到 700.9M，每个实验臂训练 8 个随机种子。通过对比三种对照配置——(1) 将分布式异构堆栈重排为均衡的周期性循环、(2) 将相同机制聚集成连续的深度带（clustering into contiguous depth bands）、(3) 用同构堆栈替换异构堆栈——来分离排列顺序与异构组成各自的贡献。此外还报告了每种机制的成本画像、中英（英/韩）双语评测，以及对全部 49 层的因果安全审计，并开源权重、训练配方、代码与日志。

Result: 在 700.9M 参数、8 种子/臂的代理实验中呈现清晰的分离效应：将分布式异构堆栈重排为均衡周期循环仅使验证损失变化 0.16%，说明精确排列位置影响极小；而把相同机制聚类为连续深度带产生 0.59% 的惩罚，用同构堆栈替换异构堆栈则产生 1.68% 的惩罚。在 2.16× 更大规模（1.514B 参数）上验证该发现：同构堆栈惩罚增至 2.63%，移除 SSM 族机制造成 3.20% 的性能退化。结果表明性能取决于异构组成在深度上的分布式布局，而非任何特定的排列。实验还包含逐机制成本画像与英、韩语评测及 49 层因果安全审计。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于以拉丁方实验设计为方法论工具，首次对"序列混合机制的异构组成 vs. 具体放置"进行因果解耦，得出性能增益主要来源于异构机制在深度上的分布式组合、而非排列顺序的结论，并辅以大规模（1.514B）验证与 SSM 机制消融。其意义在于挑战了 Transformer 逐层重复同一注意力的惯例，为异构架构搜索与深度布局设计提供指导。潜在局限：受控结论基于参数匹配的代理模型（700.9M）与更大规模验证（1.514B），尚未在完整 6.59B 规模上做多种子对比；结论的普适性仍限于所选的 4/7 种机制集合与拉丁方这一特定均衡形式，对其他机制族或非均衡排布的外推需进一步验证。

Abstract: Since GPT, most Transformers have repeated the same attention mechanism at every layer. Yet this design is largely a convention rather than a tested conclusion. When multiple sequence mixers are combined in one stack, improvements may arise from mechanism choice, placement, or both, making causal attribution difficult. We introduce Aether-7B-5Attn, a 6.59B-parameter mixture-of-experts model ($\approx$2.98B active) whose 49 layers contain seven sequence-mixing mechanisms arranged as a $7\times7$ Latin square. Because each mechanism appears exactly once in every row and column, the design guarantees balanced exposure across depth while eliminating placement confounds. To evaluate this principle, we build a parameter-matched proxy with four mechanisms arranged as a $4\times4$ Latin square over sixteen layers, matched to 700.9M parameters and trained with eight seeds per arm. The results reveal a clear dissociation. Rearranging a distributed heterogeneous stack into a balanced periodic cycle changes validation loss by only 0.16\%, indicating that exact placement has little effect. In contrast, clustering the same mechanisms into contiguous depth bands incurs a 0.59\% penalty, while replacing the heterogeneous stack with a homogeneous one incurs a 1.68\% penalty. These results indicate that performance depends primarily on heterogeneous composition distributed across depth rather than on any particular permutation. We confirm this finding at 2.16$\times$ larger scale (1.514B parameters), where the homogeneous-stack penalty increases to 2.63\% and removing the SSM-family mechanism produces a 3.20\% degradation. We further report per-mechanism cost profiles, English and Korean evaluations, and a causal-safety audit of all 49 layers. We release model weights, training recipes, training code, logs, and architecture source code.

</details>


### [292] [Labeled Incidence Structures for Native Transformer Modeling of Text, Knowledge Graphs, and Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20278)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出标记化关联结构（Labeled Incidence Structures, LIS），一种统一表示，将每个端点编码为三元组 $(x_d, s, e)$（内容、角色/槽位、所属关系实例），使文本、知识图谱、超图等异构结构数据无需展平即可被同一个标准 Transformer 原生处理。结构信息完全由算子（而非架构）承载，核心是结构地址 $A(s,e)=R_s R_e$ 与相对传输算子 $P_{j\to i}=A_i^{-1}A_j$，为注意力机制提供角色与关系感知的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本、知识图谱与超图中的元素在关系实例中扮演不同角色，各元素之间存在结构关系。将数据展平为 token 序列会丢失这些角色与结构信息。现有方法往往依赖特定架构或展平操作来适配不同数据结构，导致结构信息被破坏或无法统一建模。作者希望找到一种无需展平、能用单一标准 Transformer 处理多类结构数据的统一表示，让结构差异由算子承载而非架构。

Method: 核心表示：LIS 将每个端点表示为 $(x_d, s, e)$，其中 $x_d$ 为内容、$s$ 为角色/槽位、$e$ 为该角色所属的关系实例。结构地址由槽位算子与实例算子复合构成 $A(s,e)=R_s R_e$；论文刻画了该分解何时能为每个 token 分配唯一且路径无关的地址。当满足条件时，比较端点 $j$ 与 $i$ 的自然算子为相对传输 $P_{j\to i}=A_i^{-1}A_j$，从而赋予注意力机制角色与关系感知的归纳偏置，且不引入人为的序列顺序。论文还证明形如"位置项加关系项"的加性编码会遗漏同时依赖于 $s$ 与 $e$ 的联合信息：在受控的样例族中，当"行程算子"被近似为仅位置项与仅关系项之和时，无法刻画位置与关系如何结合，只能捕捉二者的独立效应。此外分析了持久化知识仓库：与存储位置绑定的标识符使模型对存储顺序敏感，而自由学习的标识符在仓库规模 $M$ 相对样本量 $n$ 增大时难以控制；从内容计算关系实例算子可避免存储顺序问题，并在固定架构与 Lipschitz 假设下给出与 $M$ 无关的容量界。

Result: 论文以理论分析为主：给出 $A(s,e)=R_s R_e$ 分解实现唯一、路径无关地址的刻画条件；通过在受控样例族中证明加性"位置项+关系项"编码无法表达位置与关系的联合贡献，说明此类编码的信息损失；并针对持久化知识仓库，论证基于内容计算关系实例算子可消除存储顺序敏感性，在固定架构与 Lipschitz 假设下获得与仓库规模 $M$ 无关的容量界。具体数据集、指标与数值实验未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出 LIS 统一表示与 $A(s,e)=R_s R_e$、$P_{j\to i}=A_i^{-1}A_j$ 的算子框架，使标准 Transformer 能原生处理文本、知识图谱、超图等结构数据，结构差异由算子而非架构承载；并提供加性编码局限性的理论证明及知识仓库的容量分析。意义在于为异构结构数据建模提供统一、非展平的表示与注意力归纳偏置。局限：结论多基于受控样例与固定架构、Lipschitz 等理论假设，摘要未报告大规模实证结果，实际有效性尚待验证。

Abstract: Text, knowledge graphs, and hypergraphs all have elements that play distinct roles within relation instances, structure that is lost when data is flattened into token sequences. We introduce labeled incidence structures (LIS), a uniform representation that encodes each endpoint as $(x_d, s, e)$: content $x_d$, a role or slot $s$, and the relation instance $e$ in which that role appears. Because every data type maps to the same $(x_d, s, e)$ representation without flattening, a single standard transformer can process them all natively, structural differences are carried entirely by the operators, not the architecture.
  LIS assigns a structural address to each endpoint by composing a slot operator and an instance operator, $A(s,e) = R_s R_e$. We characterize when this factorization gives every token a unique, path-independent address. When it does, the natural operator comparing endpoint $j$ to endpoint $i$ is the relative transport $P_{j\to i} = A_i^{-1} A_j$, which gives attention a role- and relation-aware inductive bias without imposing an arbitrary sequence order.
  Additive encodings of the form "position term plus relation term" can miss information that depends jointly on $s$ and $e$. We prove this in a controlled example family: when the journey operator is approximated by the sum of a position-only term and a relation-only term, the approximation cannot capture how position and relation combine, only their separate effects.
  We also analyze persistent knowledge repositories. Identifiers tied to storage locations make models sensitive to storage order, while freely learned identifiers can become harder to control as the repository size $M$ grows relative to the sample size $n$. Computing relation-instance operators from content avoids this storage-order issue and yields a capacity bound independent of $M$, under fixed architectural and Lipschitz assumptions.

</details>


### [293] [Personalising a Cross-User Surface Electromyography Encoder Under a Small Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20296)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"跨被试肌电编码器的个性化"视为一个带有代价的设计决策，在完全相同的少量标注重复样本下，系统比较了四种利用校准数据的方式（原型自适应、线性探针、缩放微调、全量微调）。核心发现是：全量微调在每个校准预算下精度最高，但无需优化器、无需保存每用户权重副本的免梯度原型规则可恢复其52%–78%的收益，使佩戴设备在佩戴瞬间即可完成个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌电（myoelectric）接口在使用前必须由用户进行校准，而先前工作多把校准当作一个"量"（即收集多少重复样本），却未追问"已收集的校准重复样本应当如何被设备使用"这一关键问题。本文认为个性化跨用户编码器是一个具有成本的设计决策——不同使用方式在精度、计算开销、存储需求与临床可实现性上代价不同。该问题对可穿戴、实时肌电控制设备的实用化至关重要，因为校准直接影响用户体验与设备能否在佩戴时即刻工作。

Method: 研究以每个留出被试的单一跨用户编码器为起点，在完全相同标注重复样本的前提下，对比四种个性化策略：（1）原型自适应（prototypical adaptation，免梯度、基于类原型的规则）；（2）线性探针（linear probes，仅训练新分类器）；（3）缩放微调（scaled fine-tuning）；（4）全量微调（full fine-tuning）。实验在每个预算（budget）下进行，直至各数据库允许的最大重复数（DB1为5次，DB2与DB5为4次）。方法学上强调在相同数据条件下做受控比较，以厘清"如何使用校准数据"而非"使用多少校准数据"的影响。

Result: 在涵盖77名被试、三个数据库（DB1、DB2、DB5）的测试中，比较四种花费小校准预算的方式：全量微调在每个预算下精度最高，且在被试子集中无一例外。关键工程结论是，免梯度的原型规则无需优化器、无需逐用户权重副本，即可恢复全量微调收益的52%–78%。此外，实验否定了"好的表征只需重新训练一个分类器即可"的普遍直觉。各方法相对于临床拟合的逐用户分类器的表现，取决于具体数据库。

Conclusion: 本文首次将肌电校准的重用方式形式化为带代价的设计决策，并通过跨77名被试、三个数据库的受控比较表明：全量微调精度最优但代价高，而免梯度原型自适应以极低成本实现可观的收益，使"佩戴即个性化"在工程上可行。其意义在于为可穿戴肌电接口的实际部署提供依据，并纠正了"固定表征+新分类器即足够"的误解。局限在于：结论仅在所测试的三个数据库与最多4–5次重复的小预算下验证；各方法相对临床级逐用户分类器的表现依赖具体数据库，泛化性有待进一步检验。

Abstract: A myoelectric interface needs calibration from the user before it will function. Earlier work has treated calibration as a quantity, but has not asked the question of what a device should do with the calibration repetitions once they have been collected. This paper views personalizing the cross-user encoder as a design decision with a cost. Four alternative approaches to using exactly the same labeled repetitions were tested from a single cross-user encoder per held-out subject. Prototypical adaptation, linear probes, scaled fine-tuning and full fine-tuning were tested at every budget up to the maximum each database allows, five repetitions on DB1 and four on DB2 and DB5. Comparing four ways to spend a small calibration budget across 77 subjects, full fine-tuning is the most accurate at every budget, consistently enough that there is no exception among subsets of subjects. The result which impacts how one might make an engineering decision however is that a gradient free prototypical rule recovers 52 to 78 per cent of its benefit with no optimiser and no per-user copy of the weights, which makes personalisation something a worn device can do at donning time. The widespread intuition that a good representation only needs a fresh classifier is incorrect here. How well each method may perform relative to a per-user classifier that would be fitted by a clinic will depend on the specific database.

</details>


### [294] [Intact-to-Amputee Transfer in Surface-EMG Gesture Decoding: Training Source and Calibration Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20297)
*Jethro Odeyemi,W. J. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对表面肌电（EMG）手势识别中的跨用户迁移问题，将一个与电极蒙太奇无关（montage-agnostic）的跨用户编码器首次系统性地迁移到11名经桡动脉截肢者上。核心发现是：零样本跨群体迁移完全失败，编码器必须借助新用户的少量有标注重复样本才能开始解码，但一旦提供三个有标注重复样本，其宏F1（0.779）即超过临床按用户定制的分类器（0.589）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMG手势识别器通常难以跨用户泛化，实际部署往往需要针对终端用户重新采集有标注数据进行校准。更关键的是，相关证据体系存在偏差：对1077项研究的系统综述显示，截肢者受试者仅出现在约六分之一的研究中，因此现有编码器的跨用户能力是否真能泛化到截肢人群缺乏实证。本文旨在检验一个跨用户编码器在经桡动脉截肢者上的迁移边界，并预注册了可证伪的预测。

Method: 方法上，作者采用一个蒙太奇无关的跨用户编码器，并将其臂部训练数据协议与截肢者测试协议对齐（即测试协议与完整肢体训练数据相匹配）。评估策略包括：零样本迁移、逐步增加有标注重复次数（如单次、三次重复）的少样本校准、以及不同源数据池的对比（40名完整受试者 vs. 10名其他截肢者，及两者的组合）。评价指标为宏F1，并对照一个临床会采用的按用户定制（per-user）分类器作为基线。作者的预注册预测在编码器“基线强度”假说之外，附加了“截肢者EMG可分性更低”的前提。

Result: 在11名经桡动脉截肢者的匹配协议上：零样本跨群体迁移彻底失败，编码器必须先获取新用户的有标注数据才能开始解码。在三次有标注重复下，编码器达到0.779宏F1，较按用户流程的0.589高出0.190，且在所有受试者上均成立。源数据池实验显示：用40名完整受试者训练对新截肢者的迁移优于用10名其他截肢者训练，而两者组合又优于任一单独来源。预注册预测仅在“少量重复样本可用且源池扩充了额外截肢者”之后才成立；在单次重复以及源数据与完整肢体比较相匹配的每一种预算下，该预测均失败。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于：一是量化了EMG跨用户/跨群体迁移研究中的证据缺口（截肢者约占六分之一）；二是通过预注册实验界定了蒙太奇无关跨用户编码器能力的边界——零样本迁移不可行，需少量标注校准；三是发现完整肢体人群数据对截肢者的迁移价值，以及多源混合的收益。意义在于为EMG神经接口的临床可用性与校准成本提供了实证依据。局限方面：结论基于11名经桡动脉截肢者的特定协议，泛化性受限；预注册预测在低预算/单一来源条件下不成立，说明其对“截肢者EMG可分性更低”这一前提的依赖较为脆弱。

Abstract: A recogniser trained on one person rarely transfers to the next, and useful performance usually demands a fresh round of labelled calibration from the end user. A systematic review of 1077 studies quantifies where the evidence is thin: amputees appear in about one in six. Here a montage-agnostic cross-user encoder is carried to eleven transradial amputees on a protocol matched to its intact-limb training data. Zero-shot cross-population transfer fails outright: the encoder requires labeled data from the new user before it begins decoding, and it then exceeds the per-user classifier a clinic would fit by 0.190 macro F1 at three repetitions and for every subject in the cohort. Given three labelled repetitions it reaches 0.779 macro-F1 against 0.589 for the per-user pipeline. Training on forty intact subjects produces better transfers to a new amputee than training on ten other amputees, and combining the two produces better transfers than either individually. The prediction pre-registered for this study, which extends the encoder's baseline-strength account with the premise that amputee EMG is less separable, holds true only after a few repetitions become available and after enriching the source pool with additional amputees. At a single repetition, and at every budget under a source matched to the intact-limb comparison, it fails. Thus, it locates the boundary of the proposed account.

</details>


### [295] [A Learning Algorithm for Threshold Boolean Networks with Prescribed Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20298)
*Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可微损失驱动的学习算法，用于从指定不动点集合推断阈值布尔网络（TBNs），并在拟南芥 FOS-GRN 模型上验证其在保持目标不动点、抑制虚假吸引子和稀疏结构方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔网络（尤其是阈值布尔网络，TBNs）是建模基因调控网络、生物动力学等系统的重要工具。在实际应用中，研究者往往已知系统需要满足的一组目标不动点（fixed points，即稳态），但直接学习或推断满足这些指定动态约束的网络结构十分困难。现有标准方法（如 Perceptron、Logistic Regression）虽然能够拟合出目标不动点，却会引入大量虚假吸引子（spurious attractors），从而破坏网络真实的动态行为。因此，如何在满足预设不动点约束的同时避免虚假吸引子、并保持网络结构稀疏，是布尔网络推断中的关键问题，对生物网络建模和系统生物学具有实际意义。

Method: 论文提出一种基于可微损失函数的学习算法来推断具有指定不动点集合的 TBNs。其核心创新在于设计了一个定制的联合损失函数，同时实现以下目标：（1）固定点保持（fixed point preservation），确保目标稳态被网络保留；（2）惩罚虚假吸引子（penalize spurious attractors），抑制非目标稳态的出现；（3）鼓励二值输出（encourage binary outputs），使网络输出符合布尔特性；（4）通过 L1 正则化促进稀疏性（promote sparsity），控制网络连接复杂度。由于损失函数可微，算法可采用基于梯度的优化方式进行训练。论文还将该方法应用于拟南芥（Arabidopsis thaliana）的 FOS-GRN 模型，并进一步分析了稀疏系数 λ 对推断网络性能与结构性质的影响。

Result: 实验在拟南芥 FOS-GRN 模型上展开，目标是恢复 10 个预设不动点且不产生虚假吸引子。在 30 次独立运行中，该方法有 5 次实现完美重建（全部 10 个目标不动点且无虚假吸引子），平均恢复 8.53 ± 0.90 个正确不动点且无虚假吸引子。作为对比，Perceptron 和 Logistic Regression 等标准方法虽可恢复多达 10 个不动点，却引入了 8 至 31 个虚假吸引子。此外，对稀疏系数 λ 的敏感性分析表明，在实用范围（最高约 0.01）内，方法的性能和推断网络的结构属性保持稳健。

Conclusion: 该论文的主要贡献在于提出了一种能够同时满足指定不动点约束、抑制虚假吸引子并保持稀疏性的可微 TBN 学习算法，并在 FOS-GRN 模型上验证了其有效性与稳定性，相较传统方法在避免虚假吸引子方面具有明显优势。其意义在于为受动态约束的布尔网络推断提供了新的优化框架。潜在局限包括：完美重建率相对较低（30 次中仅 5 次），仅在单一 FOS-GRN 模型上验证，对更大规模或更复杂网络的泛化能力尚待检验，且稀疏系数 λ 的有效范围较为有限。

Abstract: We present a learning algorithm for inferring threshold Boolean networks (TBNs) with a prescribed set of fixed points. The proposed method employs a custom differentiable loss function that jointly enforces fixed point preservation, penalizes spurious attractors, encourages binary outputs, and promotes sparsity through L1 regularization. Applied to the FOS-GRN model of Arabidopsis thaliana, the approach achieved perfect reconstruction (i.e., all 10 desired fixed points and no spurious ones) in 5 out of 30 independent runs, recovering on average 8.53 $\pm$ 0.90 correct fixed points with no spurious attractors. In contrast, standard methods such as the Perceptron and Logistic Regression recovered up to 10 fixed points but introduced between 8 and 31 spurious ones. An additional analysis varying the sparsity coefficient ($λ$) confirmed that the method's performance and the structural properties of the inferred networks remain robust within a practical range (up to 0.01) of regularization strengths. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed algorithm in capturing meaningful network dynamics under prescribed dynamical constraints.

</details>


### [296] [Improving Generalization and Robustness in Offline Reinforcement Learning via Boundary-Aware Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20300)
*Gong Gao,Weidong Zhao,Xianhui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出边界感知数据增强方法 BADA（Boundary-Aware Data Augmentation），通过利用相邻状态构建插值边界来生成更忠实于原始分布的合成数据，从而提升离线强化学习（ORL）的分布内泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离线强化学习算法容易过拟合训练数据集，部署到真实环境时分布内泛化性与鲁棒性较差。现有方法多借鉴计算机视觉中常用的正则化技术（如随机回合插值）来缓解该问题，但论文指出：低层物理信号对分布偏移高度敏感，使得这些方法在分布内泛化和鲁棒性上仍存在明显局限，难以在复杂环境中保持稳定性能。因此需要一个更契合物理信号特性的数据增强方案。

Method: 论文首先从理论上分析使用随机回合插值（random episode interpolation）训练时行为策略与动作价值函数的误差界，发现误差与状态之间的距离呈正相关。基于这一洞察，提出 BADA：利用相邻状态（neighboring states）构建插值边界，仅在边界约束内进行插值以生成合成数据，使合成样本更忠实地保留原始数据分布。方法核心在于用状态距离约束指导插值范围，从而避免生成偏离原始分布的样本，兼具策略平滑性与多模态价值分布的准确重建。

Result: 在玩具环境（toy environment）中进行定性研究，显示 BADA 生成的混合样本既保留了理想的策略平滑性，又能准确重建多模态价值分布。在多个受限离线数据集（limited offline datasets）上开展的大量实验表明，BADA 在不同基准上均达到 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: 主要贡献为：（1）从理论上揭示了随机回合插值误差与状态距离的正相关关系；（2）提出边界感知数据增强 BADA，通过相邻状态构建插值边界生成分布保真的合成数据；（3）在玩具环境和多个离线基准上验证了方法的优越性。局限在于：论文摘要未明确说明所评估基准的具体任务类型与规模，也未讨论边界构建在状态空间高维或连续控制任务中的计算开销与可扩展性。

Abstract: Current offline reinforcement learning (ORL) algorithms tend to overfit the training dataset and exhibit poor in-distribution generalization and robustness performance when deployed to real environments, thus compromising their effectiveness. Existing methods typically enhance in-distribution generalization and robustness by leveraging regularization techniques widely used in computer vision. However, due to the high sensitivity of low-level physical signals to distributional shifts, these methods still suffer from notable limitations in in-distribution generalization and robustness, making it difficult to achieve stable performance in complex environments. To address this issue, we theoretically analyze the error bounds of the behavior policy and action-value function trained with random episode interpolation, revealing that the error scales positively correlated with the distance between states. Based on this insight, we propose a method called $\bf{B}$oundary-$\bf{A}$ware $\bf{D}$ata $\bf{A}$ugmentation (BADA), which leverages neighboring states to construct interpolation boundaries, enabling the generation of synthetic data that more faithfully preserves the original data distribution. We first conduct qualitative studies in a toy environment, showing that BADA generates mixed samples that preserve desirable policy smoothness while accurately reconstructing multimodal value distributions. Extensive experiments on limited offline datasets further demonstrate that BADA attains state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [297] [SAGG: Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating for Robust Multimodal Learning under Heterogeneous Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20302)
*Wentao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Yutong Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating (SAGG)：针对多模态训练中"样本级异质污染"（同一 mini-batch 内不同样本的受损模态不同）问题，证明批次级、样本无关的共享标量梯度调制必然存在不可消除的偏差，而样本级"全有或全无"门控是自然无分布估计类中唯一无偏策略；SAGG 通过在线特征范数质量检验对每个样本做二值保留/丢弃决策，并加入截断机制控制方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态梯度平衡方法（如基于模态的梯度调制）通常为每种模态使用一个共享标量来调节编码器梯度，这隐含假设整个训练批次内的污染是均匀的。但实际中污染是样本异质的：同一 mini-batch 内，部分样本可能音频受损、部分样本可能视觉受损、部分样本可能两者完好。在这种异质污染模型下，批次级、样本无关的线性估计器会引入与干净数据梯度之间不可消除的偏差（irreducible bias），该偏差随污染程度上升而加剧。这直接影响多模态学习的稳健性与优化方向的正确性，尤其在模态缺失、噪声注入和模态贡献天然不均衡的场景（如音视频联合识别）中尤为关键。

Method: 1) 理论分析：在样本异质污染模型下，证明任何批次级、样本无关的线性估计器（共享调制参数）相对干净梯度存在不可消除偏差；并证明在自然的无分布估计器类中，样本级 all-or-nothing 门控是唯一无偏策略。2) 提出 SAGG：对每个样本进行在线特征范数质量检验，做出二值（retain / discard）门控决策，避免将受损样本的梯度混入更新。3) 引入截断（truncation）机制以控制门控带来的方差。4) 理论保证：证明基于 SAGG 的 SGD 以标准 O(1/√T) 速率收敛到干净损失的稳定点，且无与污染水平相关的误差下限（no corruption-dependent error floor）。5) 鲁棒性认证：针对独立编码器（independent-encoder）架构推导出认证鲁棒半径，将各模态 Lipschitz 常数与分类 margin 关联起来。

Result: 在 Kinetics-Sounds 与 UCF-101 两个音视频基准上，设置三类干扰：高斯噪声注入、部分模态缺失、自然贡献不均衡。SAGG 一致优于十个已有方法，且在高污染区间（批次级偏差最严重处）增益最大。理论部分给出了收敛速率（O(1/√T)，无污染相关误差下限）与独立编码器架构下的认证鲁棒半径，将逐模态 Lipschitz 常数与分类 margin 建立联系。

Conclusion: 核心贡献：(1) 揭示批次级样本无关梯度调制的固有偏差，理论上确立样本级 all-or-nothing 门控在给定估计器类中的唯一无偏性；(2) 提出 SAGG——基于在线特征范数质量检验的逐样本二值门控 + 截断方差控制；(3) 给出 O(1/√T) 收敛保证（无污染误差下限）与独立编码器架构的认证鲁棒半径；(4) 在 Kinetics-Sounds、UCF-101 的噪声、模态缺失与贡献失衡场景中超过十个基线，高污染下优势最显著。意义：为多模态稳健训练提供了从理论偏差分析到可落地算法与鲁棒性认证的完整链条。局限：方法与理论依赖"独立编码器"架构假设；质量检验基于特征范数启发式，且 all-or-nothing 门控在丢弃样本时可能损失有效信息或降低有效批大小；实验目前限于音视频识别任务，尚未验证于其他模态组合（如事件相机 + 图像）或更大规模预训练场景。

Abstract: Multimodal gradient balancing methods modulate encoder gradients with a shared scalar per modality, implicitly assuming that corruption is uniform across the training batch. In practice, corruption is sample-heterogeneous: within a single mini-batch, different samples may have different modalities corrupted. We prove that under this heterogeneous corruption model, any batch-level sample-agnostic linear estimator with a shared modulation parameter incurs an irreducible bias with respect to the clean-data gradient, and that sample-level all-or-nothing gating is the unique unbiased strategy within a natural distribution-free estimator class. Motivated by this result, we propose Sample-Adaptive Gradient Gating (SAGG), which makes a binary retain-or-discard decision per sample via an online feature-norm quality test and incorporates a truncation mechanism for variance control. We prove that SAGG-based SGD converges at the standard O(1/sqrt(T)) rate to stationary points of the clean loss without a corruption-dependent error floor, and derive a certified robustness radius for the independent-encoder architecture that connects per-modality Lipschitz constants to the classification margin. Experiments on Kinetics-Sounds and UCF-101 under Gaussian noise injection, partial modality missing, and natural contribution imbalance show that SAGG consistently outperforms ten existing methods, with the largest gains in high-corruption regimes where batch-level bias is most severe.

</details>


### [298] [Hypernetwork-Parameterized Spatially Adaptive Neural Operators for PDE Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20309)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Chaoning Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Meng Ye,Xiaofeng Zhang,Qiang He,Weifeng Huang,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Caiyan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SANO 提出一种空间自适应神经算子，用坐标条件超网络生成"随位置连续变化"的算子参数场，取代传统神经算子中空间共享的更新规则，并通过 Hyper-Neural Element（HNE）局部插值与单位分解加权，实现对空间异质 PDE 的精准建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间异质偏微分方程（如几何变化、物理系数随位置变化）具有位置依赖的动力学特性。现有神经算子的更新规则（权重/核）通常在空间上共享，虽然通过多尺度特征、注意力或域分解增强了局部建模能力，但参数本身仍不随空间变化；基于超网络的方法虽能适配不同 PDE 实例，但每个实例通常只生成一套全局参数化。结果是共享算子在边界和高梯度区域容易欠拟合，且这些局部误差会在自回归式（autoregressive）rollout 中不断累积放大。因此，如何让算子参数成为随空间连续变化的场，是一个关键且尚未解决的问题。

Method: SANO 的核心是将空间共享的参数化替换为空间连续的位置依赖算子参数场：（1）使用傅里叶编码（Fourier-encoded coordinates）表示采样点坐标，增强对高频空间变化的表达能力；（2）坐标条件超网络（coordinate-conditioned hypernetwork）在采样点上生成"空间算子条件编码"（spatial operator-conditioning codes），即每个位置对应一组算子参数；（3）提出 Hyper-Neural Element（HNE）机制，在局部子区域内对这些编码进行插值，使相邻算子相互耦合，同时允许各自的更新规则随空间变化，本质上是借鉴有限元中"单元"的局部装配思想；（4）使用单位分解（partition-of-unity）权重将重叠的局部预测进行加权聚合，保证空间覆盖的一致性与平滑拼接。整体上该设计将神经算子推广为空间自适应的连续参数场。

Result: 论文在一维、二维、三维 PDE 任务以及两个带孔洞（perforated-domain）的椭圆型基准上进行了评估。结果显示，SANO 在所有这些设置上均稳定优于竞品基线，包括神经算子类方法、基于超网络的方法以及物理信息（physics-informed）方法，验证了空间连续参数化在空间异质 PDE 建模上的有效性。摘要未给出具体误差数值与消融细节。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出空间连续的位置依赖算子参数场，以及 HNE 插值 + 单位分解聚合的局部耦合机制，缓解了共享算子在边界与高梯度区域的欠拟合及自回归误差累积问题，代表了神经算子从"实例级适配"向"空间级适配"的推进。局限在于：摘要未报告具体定量指标与计算开销；逐采样点生成参数可能带来较高的显存与推理成本；HNE 的局部子区域划分与单位分解权重的设计细节、以及方法在更强湍流/多物理耦合场景下的泛化能力仍有待验证。

Abstract: Spatially heterogeneous partial differential equations (PDEs) exhibit location-dependent dynamics arising from variations in geometry and physical coefficients. Existing neural operators improve localized modeling through multiscale features, attention mechanisms, or domain decomposition, yet their update rules often remain spatially shared. Hypernetwork-based methods adapt parameters across PDE instances but typically generate only one global parameterization per instance. Consequently, shared operators may underfit boundaries and high-gradient regions, with these localized errors accumulating during autoregressive rollout. We propose a spatially adaptive neural operator (SANO), which replaces this spatially shared parameterization with a spatially continuous field of location-dependent operator parameters. SANO uses Fourier-encoded coordinates and a coordinate-conditioned hypernetwork to generate spatial operator-conditioning codes at sampling points. A Hyper-Neural Element (HNE) mechanism interpolates these codes within local subregions, coupling neighboring operators while allowing their update rules to vary across space, and partition-of-unity weights assemble the overlapping local predictions. Experiments on one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDEs and two perforated-domain elliptic benchmarks show that SANO consistently outperforms competitive neural-operator, hypernetwork-based, and physics-informed baselines.

</details>


### [299] [ZeroHAT: Behavior-Conditioned Zero-Shot Human Activity Trace Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20310)
*Rongchao Xu,Dahai Yu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeroHAT 是一个行为条件化的零样本框架，能在目标区域缺乏真实人类活动轨迹（HAT）的情况下，从源区域迁移行为模式并结合目标区域公开上下文信息，合成目标区域的合成 HAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类活动轨迹（记录个体对兴趣点的时间戳访问）对移动性预测、城市仿真等应用至关重要，但大规模 HAT 因采集成本高、隐私敏感而难以获取。合成数据是缓解该问题的思路，但现有方法大多需要目标区域本身的真实数据来生成该区域的合成数据（同一区域依赖），对于大量没有真实 HAT 的区域不可行。因此论文提出跨区域、目标区域零样本的 HAT 合成问题。

Method: 论文提出 ZeroHAT，一个行为条件化（behavior-conditioned）框架，通过迁移源区域真实 HAT 中学到的行为模式，并利用目标区域公开可得的上下文信息进行适配，从而零样本地为目标区域生成合成 HAT。其三个关键新颖组件为：(i) 多维一致性感知意图提取器（multidimensional consistency-aware intent extractor），用于从轨迹中提取意图/行为模式；(ii) 跨区域行为克隆模块（cross-region behavioral cloning module），将源区域学到的行为迁移到目标区域；(iii) 行为条件化活动实现模块（behavior-conditioned activity realization module），在目标区域上下文条件下将行为意图落地为具体的活动轨迹。

Result: 在十城市（ten-city）基准上进行评估。实验显示 ZeroHAT 相比最强基线达到 4.5–6.4 倍的归一化下游效用（normalized downstream utility），并在各目标区域将平均保真度（average fidelity）提升 15.6%–40.8%。

Conclusion: ZeroHAT 的主要贡献在于首次面向无真实数据的区域实现零样本 HAT 合成，通过行为模式迁移与目标区域公开上下文适配，在十城市基准上显著优于现有基线（下游效用与保真度均有大幅提升），拓展了合成人类活动轨迹数据的可用范围。论文摘要未给出更多架构细节与失败案例，其局限性（如对源区域行为模式覆盖度与目标区域上下文质量的依赖）需结合全文进一步确认。

Abstract: Human activity traces record individuals' timestamped visits to points of interest and are essential for applications such as mobility prediction and urban simulation. However, accessing large-scale HATs is challenging due to high collection costs and privacy concerns. Synthetic HAT generation offers a promising way to make such data available and has attracted growing interest from both industry and academia. Although many efforts have been devoted to this topic, most of them rely on real data from a region to generate synthetic data for the same region, which is infeasible for the many regions where real HATs are unavailable. To fill this gap, we propose ZeroHAT, a behavior-conditioned framework that generates synthetic HATs for a target region in a zero-shot manner by transferring behavioral patterns learned from real HATs in source regions and adapting them with publicly available contextual information about the target region. ZeroHAT has three key novel components: (i) a multidimensional consistency-aware intent extractor; (ii) a cross-region behavioral cloning module; and (iii) a behavior-conditioned activity realization module. We evaluate ZeroHAT on a ten-city benchmark, where extensive experiments show that ZeroHAT achieves 4.5-6.4x the normalized downstream utility of the strongest baseline and improves average fidelity by 15.6%-40.8% across target regions.

</details>


### [300] [EviRec: Continual Evidence Learning for Dual Cold-Start POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20313)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 EviRec——一个面向"持续性双冷启动"POI 推荐的持续证据学习框架。作者先通过美国 10 个城市的大规模数据分析揭示 POI 更替（churn）、用户流失、类别漂移与静态 POI 记忆衰减等问题，进而提出对每个候选 POI 分别估计"历史证据可信度"的机制，在候选级别用可靠性门控自适应地在迁移记忆与生命周期证据之间路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 POI 推荐方法大多假设用户群体与 POI 目录固定不变，属于静态设定。但在真实基于位置的服务中，会出现大量新 POI 上线、老 POI 消失、用户流失与回流、类别偏好漂移，导致模型学到的历史模式迅速失效——即同时存在用户冷启动与 POI 冷启动（双冷启动），且该问题随时间持续出现（持续性）。通过 10 个美国城市的数据实证，作者量化了 POI 更替、用户周转、类别漂移和记忆衰减，指出在这一动态环境下必须解决"历史证据该被信任多少"的问题。该问题对实际 LBS 系统的在线推荐质量与长期可维护性具有直接意义。

Method: 框架核心是为每个候选 POI 单独评估历史证据的可信度，而非使用全局固定的记忆权重。EviRec 从三个互补视角对可见候选进行打分：(1) 匹配视角（matching view），基于用户近期移动性画像；(2) 迁移记忆视角（transition-memory view），刻画重复出现的移动习惯/行为模式；(3) 生命周期视角（lifecycle view），反映候选 POI 的成熟度。关键创新在于证据资格判定与门控：由于接近零的迁移分数既可能是"与用户无关"，也可能是"观测不足"，EviRec 利用每个候选的观测状态对证据加以区分，并通过可靠性门控（reliability gate）在迁移记忆证据与生命周期证据之间进行自适应路由，从而在证据不足时退回到更可靠的信息源。

Result: 在覆盖整年、5 个城市的 POI 签到数据集上评估，包含超过 30,000 名用户和 684,200 条轨迹。EviRec 一致优于当前最强的基线方法，且增益主要集中于冷启动查询：在 Dual-New 场景下 NDCG@10 相比最强基线提升 20.4%。进一步分析表明增益主要来自候选级别的可靠性门控，同时在很大程度上保留了模型先前学到的移动习惯，即持续学习过程中未出现明显的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 论文的贡献在于：(1) 通过大规模多城市实证研究揭示 POI 推荐中的持续双冷启动问题（POI 更替、用户周转、类别漂移与记忆衰减）；(2) 提出 EviRec，以候选级观测状态区分"不相关"与"观测不足"，并用可靠性门控在迁移记忆与生命周期证据间自适应路由；(3) 在整年 5 城市、30k+ 用户与 684k 轨迹的数据上显著超越 SOTA，冷启动场景收益最大。潜在局限在于：实验仅限美国城市的签到数据，跨地域与跨平台泛化性未验证；"观测状态/成熟度"的定义与门控设计可能对数据稀疏度和时间粒度敏感；论文主要报告推荐排序指标，未涉及在线服务中的计算开销与部署可行性。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation is a core task in location-based services, yet most existing methods assume a fixed user population and POI catalog. Through a large-scale data-driven analysis of 10 U.S. cities, we identify substantial POI churn, user turnover, category drift, and decay in static POI memory, motivating the study of continual dual cold-start POI recommendation. To address this setting, we propose EviRec, a continual evidence-learning framework that estimates how much historical evidence should be trusted separately for each candidate POI. EviRec scores each visible candidate from three complementary views: a matching view based on the user's recent mobility profile, a transition-memory view that captures repeated mobility routines, and a lifecycle view that reflects candidate maturity. Because a near-zero transition score may indicate either irrelevance or insufficient observation, EviRec qualifies the evidence using each candidate's observation state and applies a reliability gate to adaptively route between transition-memory and lifecycle evidence. We evaluate EviRec on a full-year, five-city POI check-in dataset containing more than 30,000 users and 684,200 trajectories. Experimental results show that EviRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with the largest gains concentrated on cold-start queries. In particular, EviRec improves NDCG@10 by 20.4\% on Dual-New cases over the strongest baseline. In-depth analyses further confirm that these gains arise primarily from candidate-specific reliability gating while largely preserving previously learned mobility routines.

</details>


### [301] [Sharp Reconstruction Bounds for Autoencoders Using the Same Forward Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20333)
*Patricia Medina,Hy P. G. Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上刻画了"在输入前后施加同一前向映射、并将观测坐标置零"的自编码器的重建误差下界，证明在保向微分同胚、Jacobian奇异值限定于[m,M]的条件下，最均匀重建导数误差恰好为 max{1-M(M-m)/2, 0}，且仿射映射在每个给定深度都能达到该紧界；并在一个79万余点的地面LiDAR森林扫描上验证了该理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自编码器（尤其是先对观测坐标置零、再经过同一前向映射的重建形式）在表示学习与压缩-重建任务中十分常见，但对其重建能力缺乏精确的量化理论。论文试图回答一个基本问题：在给定了输入/隐藏维度、映射平滑性（微分同胚）与Jacobian奇异值范围（[m,M]，即条件数）约束下，重建误差最优能小到什么程度？这一问题直接关系到压缩自编码器的表示能力边界与误差来源分析。

Method: 研究对象是"输入前后施加相同前向映射、并把观测坐标置零"的自编码器。假设输入与隐藏维度相等且为奇数 d≥3，映射属于保向微分同胚，其Jacobian奇异值落在区间 [m,M] 内。作者在"最均匀重建导数误差"这一度量下推导最优下界，并分析哪些映射类能达到该界（证明仿射映射可在任意规定深度达到）。同时给出一个反例：平移径向旋转（translated radial rotation）能对任意给定球体实现精确重建，且奇异值可任意接近1，从而说明仅靠奇异值约束不足以给出有限数据下的误差界，需要引入额外条件。最后用实验检验理论预测。

Result: 在798,452个点的地面LiDAR森林扫描数据上测试（四个空间区域、两个深度、三个随机种子）。在输入尺度0.05下，理论界的均值为0.155，约为实测平均归一化训练误差0.185的84%，理论与实验吻合较好。此外，在该尺度下增加一个隐藏坐标可将平均重建误差降至 6×10^{-6} 以下，显示额外隐藏维度能显著提升重建精度。

Conclusion: 论文给出了带零置操作的自编码器重建导数误差的紧致下界 max{1-M(M-m)/2, 0}，证明其可由仿射映射在每个深度达到，并通过LiDAR点云实验验证了该界与实际训练误差的接近程度（约84%），同时揭示了仅凭奇异值约束无法保证有限数据误差界的现象。局限在于分析针对维度相等、奇数维、保向微分同胚等较强假设，且验证仅基于单一LiDAR森林场景，未覆盖图像或事件流等其他数据类型与真实端到端网络。

Abstract: We study reconstruction in autoencoders that apply the same forward map before and after setting the observed coordinates to zero. For equal odd input and hidden dimensions $d\geq 3$, among orientation-preserving diffeomorphisms whose Jacobian singular values lie in $[m,M]$, we show that the least uniform reconstruction-derivative error is $\max\{1-M(M-m)/2,0\}$, with affine maps attaining this sharp bound at every prescribed depth. A translated radial rotation can nevertheless reconstruct any prescribed ball exactly with singular values arbitrarily close to one, motivating additional conditions for a finite-data bound. We test this prediction on a 798,452-point terrestrial LiDAR forest scan. At input scale $0.05$, the mean theoretical bound is $0.155$, about $84\%$ of the mean normalized training error $0.185$ across four spatial regions, two depths, and three seeds. At this scale, adding one hidden coordinate reduces the mean reconstruction error below $6\times10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [302] [COMPASS: Ordered Clustered Routing at 100K Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20352)
*Ido Greenberg,Hugo Linsenmaier,Piotr Sielski,Shie Mannor,Alex Fender,Gal Chechik,Eli Meirom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 COMPASS 算法，用于求解有序聚簇旅行商问题（OCTSP）。该方法将搜索与学习加速的路由相结合，通过并行协调多个子求解器，利用问题的聚簇结构实现高质量求解，并支持在大规模、非对称距离矩阵场景下的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模路由（如电商配送、物流规划）中常需要按指定顺序访问节点簇，这构成 OCTSP。独立优化每个簇看似自然，但会忽略簇间的非局部依赖关系，导致次优解。同时，现有大规模路由求解器通常局限于坐标输入，难以处理一般的（尤其非对称的）距离矩阵，且缺乏可随算力持续改进的求解质量保证。

Method: COMPASS 将搜索与学习加速的路由相结合，通过编排（orchestrate）并行子求解器协同求解。其关键创新在于：(1) 利用问题的聚簇结构，使精确求解的时间复杂度随簇大小而非整体实例规模指数增长；(2) 无质量上限（no quality ceiling），解的质量可随计算资源增加而持续提升；(3) 直接消费通用距离矩阵，不依赖坐标输入，因此可处理非对称距离（ATSP）场景。

Result: 实验表明 COMPASS 持续优于对比方法。作者在 100K 合成节点上验证了可扩展性，并在真实电商场景下求解了 28.5K 节点的问题。据作者所述，这是目前报道的最大规模非对称距离路由解，超出既有 ATSP 基准约 9 倍。评价主要围绕解质量与可扩展性展开。

Conclusion: COMPASS 为 OCTSP 提出了一种可扩展、可随算力提升质量、且支持通用距离矩阵的求解框架，在合成与真实大规模非对称路由问题上取得显著规模突破。其核心贡献是结合聚簇结构利用与并行学习加速搜索。潜在局限在于：学习组件与并行子求解器的协调开销、 真实电商数据的具体质量指标（如与下界或精确解的差距）未在摘要中量化，以及精确求解的指数复杂度仍受簇大小约束。

Abstract: Large-scale routing often requires visiting clusters of nodes in a prescribed order, giving rise to the Ordered Clustered Traveling Salesman Problem (OCTSP). Optimizing each cluster independently seems natural, but misses non-local dependencies. We introduce the COMPASS algorithm for OCTSP, which combines search with learning-accelerated routing by orchestrating parallel sub-solvers. COMPASS has no quality ceiling and its solutions keep improving with compute. It exploits the clustered structure, and can reach exact solutions in time exponential in cluster size rather than instance size. Empirically, COMPASS consistently outperforms alternative methods. Unlike common large-scale routing solvers, COMPASS consumes general distance matrices and is not limited to coordinate inputs. We demonstrate scaling to 100K synthetic nodes and to 28.5K real e-commerce nodes. To our knowledge, the latter is the largest reported routing solution over asymmetric distances, 9x beyond established ATSP benchmarks.

</details>


### [303] [Minimax-Optimal Online Contract Design with Unrestricted Bounded Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20353)
*Rui Ai,David Simchi-Levi,Han Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"重复契约设计"（repeated contract design）问题：委托人只观测结果、无法观测代理人动作，在每轮可用任意有界的结果依赖支付向量的设定下，刻画了极小极大遗憾（minimax regret）随回合数 T 与可契约结果数 m 的精确依赖关系，对每个固定 m≥2 给出 Θ̃(T^{m/(m+1)}) 的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在契约设计中，委托人只能观察结果而非生成结果的动作，代理人会针对支付向量做最优反应，这会使期望利润对支付向量产生不连续性（代理人可通过离散的切换行为"跳变"），从而传统基于平滑性或单调剩余假设的学习理论难以直接适用。作者关注的核心动机是：当契约可依赖的结果维度 m 增多时，学习最优契约的最坏情况代价究竟如何变化？这一"维度-代价"权衡此前缺乏精确刻画。该问题的意义在于为信息不对称、委托-代理场景下的在线/重复学习提供理论下界与可达速率，避免依赖不现实的平滑假设。

Method: 技术路线分为上界与下界两部分。上界方面：(1) 提出"有效维度归约"（effective-dimension reduction），证明即使固定打破平局的规则不具备平移不变性时，基准（benchmark）仍可被归一化；(2) 在此基础上，利用显示偏好（revealed preference）把代理人的响应在"支付差坐标"（payment-difference coordinates）中刻画为单调响应映射；(3) 对该单调映射施加 Lipschitz 参数化，构建仅依赖观测到的结果类别（outcome categories）的学习策略，达到 T^{m/(m+1)} 速率（忽略对数因子）。该框架允许任意动作空间与代理人异质性，无需平滑性或单调剩余（monotone-surplus）假设。下界方面：构造了刻画激励损失沿不同结果维度累加的对抗性实例，证明每增加一个可契约结果维度都会带来精确且不可避免的学习代价上升，从而匹配上界速率（至对数因子）。

Result: 论文为每个固定 m≥2 建立了极小极大遗憾的紧界 Θ̃(T^{m/(m+1)})：上界由基于支付差坐标中单调 Lipschitz 响应映射的策略取得，下界由考虑激励损失跨结果维度累积的构造给出。主要发现是：(1) 可契约结果数 m 同时决定遗憾对 T 的指数 m/(m+1) 与常数层面的难度，维度越高学习越慢；(2) 在不假设平滑性的前提下仍能得到多项式速率，说明结构（单调性/Lipschitz 化）来自问题本身而非假设；(3) 学习策略仅需观测到结果类别，无需动作信息，符合委托-代理的可观测量约束。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于给出重复契约设计中以可契约结果数 m 为参数的精确极小极大速率，提出有效维度归约与支付差坐标下的单调 Lipschitz 参数化方法，并在最少假设（任意动作空间、代理人异质、无平滑性）下证明可达性与不可避免性。其意义在于揭示"多维度契约"对学习效率的系统性代价，并为信息不对称下的重复学习提供理论基准。局限可能包括：结论围绕固定 m 的最坏情况率，对 m 随 T 增长或结果空间连续时的刻画未在摘要中体现；策略的常数因子与对数因子、计算可实现性，以及对代理人更复杂（非理性或有限理性）行为的鲁棒性仍有待讨论。

Abstract: We study repeated contract design when a principal observes outcomes but not the actions that generate them. The principal may use any bounded outcome-contingent payment vector, and the agent's best response can make expected profit discontinuous in those payments. For every fixed number $m\ge2$ of outcomes, the minimax regret over $T$ rounds is of order $T^{m/(m+1)}$, up to logarithmic factors. The upper bound allows arbitrary action spaces and agent heterogeneity, without smoothness or monotone-surplus assumptions. Its key is an effective-dimension reduction that the benchmark can be normalized even when fixed tie-breaking is not shift invariant, after which revealed preference yields a monotone response map in payment-difference coordinates. A learning policy built on a Lipschitz parametrization of this map attains the rate using only observed outcome categories. The lower-bound construction accounts for how incentive losses accumulate across outcome dimensions. It shows that each additional contractible outcome creates a precise and unavoidable increase in the worst-case cost of learning.

</details>


### [304] [Learning Principal-Agent Contracts for Equitable Smallholder Carbon Farming under Moral Hazard and Adverse Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20404)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Yadati Narahari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从**契约设计（contract design）**视角研究碳农业（carbon farming）为何难以覆盖小农户。作者将聚合商（aggregator）面向异质性小农户的动态契约问题建模为**POMDP**，用**强化学习**学习利润最大化的动态契约，发现利润导向的聚合商会**放大而非继承**对小农户的排斥（大农场采纳率达 87.7%，小农仅 8.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业土壤是巨大的未开发碳汇，碳农业（carbon farming）是释放该潜力的重要实践，而南亚和撒哈拉以南非洲以小农户为主体的农业结构是规模化气候减缓的关键。然而现实中碳项目基本无法触达小农户，形成显著落差。本文关注的核心问题是：这一排斥现象是市场/契约结构的内生结果，还是可以通过契约与政策设计加以纠正？为何这一问题重要——若排斥是契约机制内生的，则仅靠补贴式干预可能无效，必须从契约激励结构入手。

Method: 建模框架：将聚合商与异质性农户之间的**演化式契约问题（evolving contracting problem）**形式化为**部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）**，其中包含两类经典信息经济学摩擦——农户拥有私人采纳成本（**逆向选择 / adverse selection**）与不可观测的努力投入（**道德风险 / moral hazard**），且农艺产出（agronomic outcomes）跨多个季节演化。求解方式：采用**强化学习**学习动态、利润最大化的契约策略，聚合商向整个异质性农户群体提供**单一 pooled contract（池化契约）**。分析维度：在不同条件下评估聚合商表现，并构造**反事实实验**——将 MRV（Measurement, Reporting and Verification，测量、报告与核查）成本设为纯面积比例（purely area-proportional），检验该改动能否消除大小农户间的采纳差距。

Result: 主要发现：（1）利润最大化的聚合商不只是继承小农户被排斥的现状，而是**放大这种排斥**；（2）大农场的采纳率达到可实现采纳量的 **87.7%**，而小农地块仅为 **8.2%**；（3）三大机制：单位面积 MRV 成本随农场规模增大而下降，而聚合商的**池化契约进一步强化**这一规模梯度，而非抵消它；（4）反事实分析显示，若使 MRV 成本为纯面积比例，则**该采纳差距可以被消除**。评估主要基于仿真结果。

Conclusion: 贡献与意义：本文首次以契约设计+RL 的 POMDP 框架系统量化碳农业中小农户被排斥的内生机制，指出池化契约与 MRV 成本规模效应共同放大不平等，并给出可操作的 MRV 成本结构改革方向（面积比例定价）作为政策启示，为面向小农户的碳收入与大规模土壤碳减缓提供设计依据。局限：结论基于仿真而非真实田野数据，未在摘要中给出具体参数设定与实证验证；MRV 成本测度较为简化，对真实农艺随机性、农户行为异质性与制度环境的刻画可能受限。

Abstract: Agricultural soils are a major untapped carbon sink. Carbon farming is emerging as a promising practice for tapping this potential. Smallholder farmers, who dominate agriculture across South Asia and sub-Saharan Africa, are key to scaling climate mitigation via carbon farming. It is ironic that real-world carbon programs largely fail to reach them. We study this important gap through the lens of contract design. An aggregator offers a single pooled contract to a heterogeneous population of smallholder farmers who have private adoption costs (adverse selection) and exert unobserved effort (moral hazard), with agronomic outcomes evolving over multiple seasons. We formulate this evolving contracting problem as a POMDP and use reinforcement learning to learn a dynamic profit-maximising contract. We analyse the performance of the aggregator under various conditions. We find that a profit-maximising aggregator does not merely inherit the exclusion of smallholders, it amplifies it. On large farms the aggregator realises 87.7% of achievable adoption, against only 8.2% on smallholdings. Per-hectare Measurement, Reporting and Verification (MRV) costs fall as farm size rises, and the aggregator's pooling contract compounds this gradient rather than offsetting it. A counterfactual that makes MRV costs purely area-proportional eliminates this disparity. Our results and simulation can guide contract and policy design that opens carbon income to smallholders while enabling agricultural soils to contribute to climate mitigation at scale.

</details>


### [305] [The Bias of Nonlinear Two-Time-scale Stochastic Approximation under Constant Step-Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20409)
*Djamel Rassem Lamouri,Dorian Baudry,Nicolas Gast*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究步长满足 $\\alpha \\gg \\beta$ 的非线性两时间尺度随机逼近（TTSA），在标准稳定性、正则性与马尔可夫噪声假设下给出两个迭代变量围绕其极限平衡点的均方误差与偏差的有限时间上界，界的形式为 $O(\\alpha+\\beta^2/\\alpha^2)$，并证明当 $\\beta\\le\\alpha^{3/2}$ 时该界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两时间尺度随机逼近是分析强化学习、优化与随机控制中耦合迭代算法的基本工具。已有工作多聚焦线性 TTSA 的有限时间分析，而非线性两时间尺度方案的有限时间保证仍难以获得，尤其在固定步长（constant step-size）情形下更为困难。理解非线性带来的有限时间效应，对于判断这类算法在实际中的收敛速度与稳定性具有重要意义。

Method: 论文考察步长 $\\alpha \\gg \\beta$ 的非线性 TTSA 迭代。在标准稳定性、正则性以及马尔可夫噪声假设下，分别对慢时间尺度与快时间尺度迭代量的均方误差及其相对于极限平衡点的偏差进行上界推导。分析将误差来源显式分解为初始条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差（tracking error）、马尔可夫依赖以及时间尺度耦合四部分，从而说明 $\\beta^2/\\alpha^2$ 项的产生机理；并通过构造性论证证明在 $\\beta\\le\\alpha^{3/2}$ 时该上界是紧的，同时与线性 TTSA 的既有结果进行定性对比，指出非线性动力学引入了线性情形中不存在的额外有限时间效应。

Result: 实验部分为理论分析：在标准假设下得到均方误差与偏差的有限时间上界 $O(\\alpha+\\beta^2/\\alpha^2)$，并证明在条件 $\\beta\\le\\alpha^{3/2}$ 下该阶数是紧的。分析明确分离了初始条件、快时间尺度跟踪误差、马尔可夫噪声依赖与时间尺度耦合的各自贡献。主要发现是与线性 TTSA 相比，非线性 TTSA 在有限时间层面表现出额外的效应，即 $\\beta^2/\\alpha^2$ 项的出现与耦合机制密切相关。

Conclusion: 该工作为固定步长下的非线性 TTSA 提供了首个（或更完整的）有限时间均方误差与偏差界，并给出界紧性的条件，澄清了 $\\beta^2/\\alpha^2$ 项的来源，揭示了非线性相较于线性的定性差异。其意义在于为强化学习与随机控制中非线性两时间尺度算法的收敛速率分析提供理论工具。潜在局限在于结果依赖于标准稳定性、正则性与马尔可夫噪声假设，且紧性仅在 $\\beta\\le\\alpha^{3/2}$ 条件下成立，未覆盖更一般的时间尺度分离比例。

Abstract: Two-timescale stochastic approximation (TTSA) is a fundamental tool for analyzing coupled iterative algorithms in reinforcement learning, optimization, and stochastic control. However, finite-time guarantees for nonlinear two-timescale schemes remain difficult to obtain, especially under constant step-sizes. In this paper, we study nonlinear TTSA with step-sizes $α\ggβ$. Under standard stability, regularity, and Markovian noise assumptions, we upper bound the mean-squared error and the bias of both iterates around their limiting equilibria. Our bounds scale as $O(α+β^2/α^2)$, which we prove to be tight when $β\leα^{3/2}$. The analysis separates the contributions of initial conditions, fast-timescale tracking error, Markovian dependence, and timescale coupling, thereby clarifying the origin of the $β^2/α^2$ term. Our results reveal qualitative differences from the linear TTSA setting previously studied, showing that nonlinear dynamics introduce additional finite-time effects that are absent in the linear case.

</details>


### [306] [SCGFM-ART: Amortized Relational Transport for Structure-Centric Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20419)
*Xiaodong He,Xincheng Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCGFM-ART 提出以结构为中心的图基础模型框架，通过可摊销关系传输（Amortized Relational Transport, ART）将任意图对齐到由有限关系地标（基）构成的共享"关系图集"（relational atlas）上，从而绕开昂贵的运行时 Gromov-Wasserstein 优化，实现跨异构图域的统一、域无关表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFM）的目标是跨高度异构的图域学习可迁移表示。然而，不同图在拓扑结构、图规模以及特征语义上存在严重的域偏移（domain shift），这阻碍了统一的、域无关表示空间的构建。已有方法往往依赖代价高昂的 Gromov-Wasserstein 等最优传输优化在测试时进行迭代对齐，导致推理效率低下。因此，作者需要一种既能解决结构/语义异质性、又能避免测试时高开销对齐的统一建模方案。

Method: 核心是把图与图集基严格建模为有限测度关系空间（finite measured relational spaces），并引入"关系图集"作为由一组有限关系地标（基）定义的通用坐标系。ART 直接预测可复用、端到端的图到基的传输计划（transport plans），替代运行时迭代求解 Gromov-Wasserstein。在此框架下，图被分解为统一的双层表示：全局层面通过相对图集的关系响应坐标（relational response coordinates）表征；局部层面通过节点到角色（node-to-role）的结构对应关系表征，并将异构节点属性投影到规范角色空间（canonical role space），在单一对齐接口中同时消解结构与语义异质性。理论上建立了坐标保真度界，证明了在预测传输计划下的稳定性，并推导出摊销覆盖界，保证学习目标紧密近似理想的关系覆盖。

Result: 在 14 个跨域图级与节点级分类任务上进行评测，SCGFM-ART 取得最优迁移性，图级与节点级平均排名分别为 2.29 和 1.14。拓扑扰动分析表明，节点-角色传输能保留超出全局坐标的细粒度结构信息。在真实世界基准上，摊销式公式使冻结目标域推理相比测试时迭代对齐加速 44.2 至 85.1 倍。

Conclusion: SCGFM-ART 的主要贡献在于：以结构为中心的关系图集设想、可摊销的关系传输机制（无需测试时迭代对齐）、统一的全局坐标+局部角色双层表示，以及配套的坐标保真、稳定性与覆盖理论保证，并在跨域迁移与推理效率上均取得领先表现。其潜在局限在于：图集基（地标）的选择与数量可能影响覆盖质量与泛化性；方法重点在结构对齐，特征语义的处理依赖于角色空间的规范化，对极端语义异质性或超大规模图的适用性仍待进一步验证（文中未展开说明）。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable representations across severely heterogeneous graph domains. However, severe domain shifts in topology, graph scale, and feature semantics impede the construction of a unified, domain-agnostic representation space. To address this, we propose SCGFM-ART, a structure-centric GFM framework that aligns arbitrary graphs onto a shared relational atlas via Amortized Relational Transport (ART). The relational atlas serves as a universal coordinate system defined by a finite set of relational landmarks (bases), while ART directly predicts reusable, end-to-end graph-to-base transport plans, bypassing costly runtime Gromov-Wasserstein optimizations. Under this formulation, SCGFM-ART decomposes a graph into a unified representation: globally via its relational response coordinates relative to the atlas, and locally via its node-to-role structural correspondences. These correspondences project disparate node attributes into a canonical role space, resolving structural and semantic heterogeneity within a singular alignment interface. Rigorously modeling graphs and atlas bases as finite measured relational spaces, we establish coordinate fidelity bounds, prove stability under predicted transport plans, and derive an amortized coverage bound that guarantees our learning objective tightly surrogates ideal relational coverage. Benchmarked across 14 cross-domain graph- and node-level classification tasks, SCGFM-ART achieves state-of-the-art transferability, securing superior average ranks of 2.29 and 1.14, respectively. Topological perturbation analyses demonstrate that node-role transport retains fine-grained structural nuances beyond global coordinates. On real-world benchmarks, the amortized formulation yields 44.2 to 85.1 times faster frozen target-domain inference by avoiding iterative alignment at test time.

</details>


### [307] [Seismic Site Response Prediction from Sparse Observations Using Finite-Element-Pretrained Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20451)
*Yi Zhu,Su Chen,Xiaojun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLARE-T（Transfer-Enabled Forced Latent Autoencoder for Response Equations）通过从稠密有限元模拟中学习低维响应流形与输入驱动动力学，再用稀疏编码器将少量实测传感器响应映射到该潜空间进行校准，从而在数据受限条件下改进多深度场地响应预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值场地响应预测（如分层土体在不同深度处的加速度响应）常与实测记录存在偏差。校正这些偏差面临双重数据瓶颈：一是传感器空间覆盖稀疏（仅能在有限的深度/位置布设传感器），二是可用的地震事件记录数量有限。传统方法难以在如此稀缺的实测数据下有效校准高维、复杂的数值模型，因此需要一种数据高效的方法，将稠密数值模拟信息与有限现场记录相结合。

Method: FLARE-T 采用潜空间动力学学习与迁移校准的思路，核心分三步：1）从稠密有限元模拟中学习低维响应流形，并建立由基底加速度输入驱动的潜空间动力学（输入到多深度输出的映射）；2）训练一个稀疏编码器，将模拟的稀疏传感器响应映射到已学得的潜坐标中，实现仿真域与稀疏观测域的对齐；3）利用有限的实测记录在潜空间内校准动力学参数，而非直接校准高维物理参数。预测时，使用一段短响应窗口进行初始化，随后由完整的基底运动驱动整个响应序列的生成。

Result: 评估使用两个数据集/场景：分层土离心机试验（centrifuge test）与 Lotung 现场竖向台阵（vertical array）。评价指标包括多深度加速度时程（acceleration histories）和 5% 阻尼伪加速度反应谱（pseudoacceleration response spectra）。结果表明：1）相比原始有限元模型，FLARE-T 在所有评估传感器位置上均降低了误差，且适用于不同强度的地震动；2）在 Lotung 场地上，对水平两个分量均有改善；3）使用两套不同本构参数（constitutive parameters）的 Lotung 源模型仍能得到相当的测试集精度，说明该方法降低了对精确先验标定的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献在于提出了一种数据高效的场地响应预测框架，通过潜空间流形学习与稀疏编码器迁移，将稠密数值模拟与有限实测记录有效融合，并在离心机试验和现场台阵上验证了其相对于有限元基线的改进，且表现出对本构参数先验的鲁棒性。潜在局限包括：方法依赖高质量的有限元模拟作为先验来源；稀疏编码器与潜动力学的校准效果受限于可用记录数量与传感器覆盖；目前验证局限于场地响应（地震工程）场景，跨场地或强非线性情形下的泛化能力仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Numerical site-response predictions often deviate from observations, yet correcting these discrepancies is difficult because records are limited in both sensor coverage and number of events. This study proposes the Transfer-Enabled Forced Latent Autoencoder for Response Equations (FLARE-T) to improve these predictions by learning and calibrating low-dimensional latent dynamics that connect the base acceleration input to acceleration outputs at multiple depths. FLARE-T learns a low-dimensional response manifold and input-driven dynamics from dense finite-element simulations. It then trains a sparse encoder to map simulated sensor responses into the learned coordinates and uses limited records to calibrate the dynamics within them. A short response window initializes each prediction, while the complete base motion drives the response. The framework was evaluated using a layered-soil centrifuge test and the Lotung field vertical array. Test-set results show that FLARE-T improved multi-depth acceleration histories and 5%-damped pseudoacceleration response spectra relative to the original finite-element models, reducing errors at every evaluated sensor for motions of different intensities and, at Lotung, for both horizontal components. Two Lotung source models with different constitutive parameters achieved comparable test-set accuracy, indicating reduced dependence on precise prior calibration. FLARE-T therefore provides a data-efficient means of combining dense numerical response information with limited field records to improve future site-response predictions.

</details>


### [308] [Training Neural Networks to Approach the Optimum Bayes Estimator in Dense Multi-Emitter Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20465)
*Yi Sun,Mona Sharifi,Muzna Yumman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作通过在合成帧上训练神经网络，逼近密集发光体定位问题的贝叶斯最优估计器，并以此论证未来通过神经网络实现高通量、大视场、时空超分辨SMLM（单分子定位显微镜）的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集发光体定位是SMLM等超分辨成像任务的核心难点。在发射体密度较高时，传统单分子定位方法受限于发光体重叠，难以准确分辨和定位，估计精度远离理论极限。贝叶斯最优估计器（Bayes estimator）给出了该问题在给定成像模型下的理论精度上限（如CRLB所界定的界），但通常计算上不可行。作者由此提出用神经网络学习该最优估计器，以突破传统方法的精度与通量瓶颈，并为高通量、大视场、时空超分辨SMLM奠定基础。

Method: 以合成帧（synthesized frames）作为监督训练数据，即依据成像前向模型（PSF、噪声统计、发光体分布等）生成带真值的模拟帧，采用监督学习方式训练神经网络直接回归发光体位置（密集定位）。核心思想是将神经网络视为对贝叶斯最优估计器的函数逼近，使网络在统计意义上逼近理论最优定位精度，从而避免显式求解复杂的贝叶斯推断。方法层面强调数据合成策略与估计器逼近，而非特定网络结构细节。

Result: 实验以合成帧为训练与评估基础，将神经网络定位性能与贝叶斯最优估计器（理论上界）进行对比，验证了神经网络能够逼近该最优估计器，从而在密集发光体条件下获得接近理论极限的定位表现。该结果为后续将神经网络用于实现高通量、大视场、时空超分辨SMLM提供了依据。具体数据集规模、网络结构与量化指标在摘要中未给出。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明神经网络可通过合成帧训练逼近密集发光体定位的贝叶斯最优估计器，从而为基于学习的高通量、大视场超分辨SMLM提供理论和方法支撑。其意义在于将计算上难以处理的贝叶斯最优推断转化为可训练、可扩展的神经估计器，有望提升成像通量与视场。局限在于当前工作主要基于合成数据验证，尚未在真实SMLM数据上验证其泛化性与实际性能，摘要也未给出具体网络结构、评估指标与定量结果。

Abstract: We train neural networks on synthesized frames to approach the optimum Bayes estimator for dense emitter localization. The result justifies the future work on training neural networks to achieve high-throughput large-FOV super spatiotemporal resolution SMLM.

</details>


### [309] [Deep Learning-Based Classification of Cognitive and Resting States Using Electroencephalography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20467)
*K. A. Januka S. Fernando,Harshit Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合CNN与GRU的深度学习框架（2D-Net），配合时频分析特征提取，用于区分EEG信号中的静息状态与认知状态（数学、记忆、音乐任务），在三类二分类任务上均取得优于对比方法的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的静息态与认知态分类对于理解大脑活动波动及相关心理状态具有重要意义。EEG提供非侵入式的脑功能记录手段，但信号本身复杂、非平稳，传统方法难以自动提取有判别力的模式。该研究旨在利用深度学习自动挖掘EEG中的显著特征，实现静息态与任务导向认知态的有效区分，为脑机接口与神经科学分析提供支持。

Method: 框架核心为CNN堆叠GRU的2D-Net架构：CNN负责从EEG信号中提取空间/局部特征，GRU用于建模时间序列上的时序依赖。方法首先对EEG信号进行时频分析以挖掘显著特征，随后将提取的特征输入所提网络，并与常规深度学习及传统机器学习分类器进行对比评估。整体思路将信号处理（时频表征）与深度学习（时空特征学习）相结合。

Result: 在三类静息-认知对照任务上进行二分类评估：静息 vs 数学任务准确率83.177%，静息 vs 记忆任务76.107%，静息 vs 音乐任务83.432%。所提方法与特征提取策略均优于所评估的对比方法，表明时频特征与CNN-GRU组合在该任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提出CNN+GRU的2D-Net框架并结合时频分析，验证了信号处理与深度学习结合用于EEG静息/认知态判别的有效性，在多类任务上取得较优准确率。局限方面：摘要未说明数据集规模与来源、跨被试泛化能力、时序建模计算开销及统计显著性验证，且记忆任务准确率相对偏低，泛化性与实时性有待进一步评估。

Abstract: The categorization of cognitive and resting states derived from electroencephalography (EEG) signals is crucial for comprehending fluctuations in brain activity linked to various mental states. EEG provides a non-intrusive approach for documenting brain function in both resting and task-oriented cognitive conditions, whilst deep learning techniques enable the automatic extraction of significant patterns from intricate EEG data. This study presents a deep learning framework to distinguish between resting and cognitive states through EEG records. The proposed framework integrates a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) stacked with a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) for the extraction of features from EEG signals. Time-frequency analysis is conducted to explore the salient aspects of signals, and the derived features are then assessed utilizing conventional deep learning and machine learning classifiers, including the suggested 2D-Net architecture. The proposed approach and feature extraction strategy outperform the evaluated comparative methods, achieving accuracies of 83.177% for resting-versus-mathematical task classification, 76.107% for resting-versus-memory task classification, and 83.432% for resting-versus-music task classification. The findings illustrate the efficacy of integrating signal processing with deep learning methodologies to discriminate resting from cognitive states utilizing EEG signals.

</details>


### [310] [Distributionally Robust Federated Learning with Multi-Source Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20501)
*Yingzhu Liu,Zhongkui Li,Pengcheng You,Ashish Cherukuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对联邦学习中数据分布不确定性问题，提出以"局部模糊集的可行混合的并集"构建全局模糊集，兼顾跨客户端混合权重不确定与客户端内分布模糊，并给出可客户端分解的重构、高概率泛化保证及收敛的联邦算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习从私有客户端数据训练共享模型，但实际中客户端数据生成分布存在异质性（非IID），且真实客户端混合比例往往未知，导致底层群组分布难以准确刻画。现有方法多通过最坏情况混合优化来处理跨客户端混合不确定性，但隐含假设了各客户端的分布估计是准确的；当这些估计仅基于有限样本时并不可靠。因此需要在同时考虑跨客户端混合不确定性（外部不确定性）与客户端内分布歧义（内部不确定性、有限样本误差）的统一框架下进行鲁棒优化。

Method: - 全局模糊集构建：将全局模糊集定义为各局部（客户端级）模糊集的可容许混合的并集（union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets），从而同时刻画跨客户端混合权重不确定性与客户端内分布歧义，并允许客户端各自的模糊半径（client-specific ambiguity radii）。
- 结构性质：该构造具有客户端可分解（client-wise separable）的重构形式，便于分布式/联邦式求解。
- 理论保证：利用上述结构建立高概率的样本外（out-of-sample）性能保证。
- 算法：针对惩罚式（penalty-based）重构发展联邦优化算法，并在较温和的正则性条件下证明其收敛性。
- 实验：通过仿真验证算法有效性。

Result: 论文以仿真实验评估所提联邦算法，验证其有效性。理论方面给出了高概率样本外性能保证，并在比已有工作更弱的正则性条件下证明了惩罚式重构联邦算法的收敛性。摘要未披露具体数据集、基线方法、评价指标及定量结果。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于：提出以局部模糊集混合并集构造全局模糊集的统一鲁棒联邦学习框架，同时处理跨客户端混合不确定性与客户端内分布歧义，支持客户端特定模糊半径；给出客户端可分解重构、高概率泛化界与收敛性证明。意义在于将分布鲁棒优化与联邦异质性建模结合，缓解有限样本下客户端分布估计不可靠的问题。局限：验证仅限仿真，缺乏真实联邦数据集与大规模实验；未与现有最坏情况混合方法进行定量对比；摘要未说明模糊集的具体形式（如Wasserstein球）与计算复杂度。

Abstract: Federated learning trains a shared model from private client data. In practice, data-generating distributions may differ, and the true mixture across clients is often unknown, making the underlying group distribution difficult to specify. Existing approaches address cross-client mixture uncertainty by optimizing against the worst-case mixture, yet assume accurate client-wise distribution estimates. However, these estimates can be unreliable when based on finite samples. To handle both cross-client mixture uncertainty and within-client distributional ambiguity, we construct a global ambiguity set as the union of admissible mixtures of local ambiguity sets. The construction allows client-specific ambiguity radii and admits a client-wise separable reformulation. Leveraging this structure, we establish a high-probability out-of-sample performance guarantee. We further develop a federated algorithm for a penalty-based reformulation and prove its convergence under milder regularity conditions. Simulations validate the algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [311] [Radio Frequency Detection and Classification of Microplastics in Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20507)
*Jaden Tolbert,Md Saiful Islam,Pingshan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种机器学习辅助的射频介电谱流式细胞术（RF DiSC）平台，利用微波频段（0.2–9 GHz）散射参数（S-parameters）对水溶液中的微/纳米塑料颗粒进行无标记检测与分类。


<details>
